## situation.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘迷宫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

The Mysterious Labyrinth

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:85`

## situation.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们位于[This.GetName]的殖民者有了瞠目结舌的发现。在偏僻的山脉深处，他们碰巧撞见了一座神秘的结构，根据我们的扫描，这座建筑在我们第一次调查[This.GetPlanetMoon]的时候还不在那里。它的状态完好无损，就像是刚刚建造的一样，但是没有证据表明[Owner.GetAdj]参与了建造。事实上，它的构型显示出种种异星特征，曲边和拱角非常的不寻常，完全有悖于我们的美学感知。\n\n在它的入口，有一个标志，上面写着（很神奇，用的是我们的语言）：“欢迎来到壮阔秀丽的跨次元异常点迷宫。进入者自行承担风险。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

Our colonists on [This.GetName] have made a startling discovery. Deep in a remote mountain range, they stumbled across a mysterious structure that, according to our scans, was not there when we first surveyed the [This.GetPlanetMoon]. Its condition is excellent, almost as if it was newly built, but there is no evidence of [Owner.GetAdj] involvement in its construction. In fact, its architecture shows alien characteristics, with curves and angles that appear unusual and even disturbing to our aesthetic sensibilities.\n\nAt its entrance, there is a sign (in our language, curiously) with the words "Welcome to the Magnificent Labyrinth of Exotic Trans-Dimensional Anomalies. Enter at your own risk."

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:86`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.5.A&lang=en)

### 中文

派一支队伍前去调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

Send a team to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:87`

## situation.10.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.10.name&lang=en)

### 中文

惊奇的活动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

Surprising Movements

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:90`

## situation.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看上去[Target.GetName]上的这个异星迷宫在试图吸引我们的注意。我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]很不情愿将这么一个事物归为一栋建筑，但是恐怕我们只能这么认为：迷宫现在不再坐落于遥远的群山之中，而是距离我们的一个主要居住点很短的距离。一天接一天，它令人费解地变换着自己的位置，而且其入口的那个警告标志也消失了——事实上，它看上去好像……在邀请我们。\n\n尽管有些好奇宝宝忍不住头铁进去了，我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]还是劝告谨慎对待。唯一清楚的是，我们不能再满足于从远处观察迷宫了——必须采取新的方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

It appears that the alien labyrinth on [Target.GetName] is attempting to attract our attention. Our [Owner.GetResearchers] are loath to ascribe such agency to a building, but that is the only way to put it: the labyrinth is now no longer situated in a remote mountain pass, but is now instead located a short distance from one of our main settlements. From one day to the next, it inexplicably shifted its location, and the warning signs at the entrance have disappeared - in fact, it now looks... inviting.\n\nWhile some are curious to enter, our [Owner.GetResearchers] urge caution. What is clear, however, is that we can no longer content ourselves with observing the labyrinth - a new approach will be necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:91`

## situation.10.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.10.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

看上去[Target.GetName]上的这个异星迷宫在试图吸引我们的注意。尽管一栋建筑会有任何形式的意识这一点看上去很不可思议，但是恐怕我们只能这么认为：迷宫现在不再坐落于遥远的群山之中，而是距离我们的一个主要存储区域很短的距离。一天接一天，它令人费解地变换着自己的位置，而且其入口的那个警告标志也消失了——事实上，它看上去好像……在邀请我们。\n\n这一改变的出现，会让我们阻止[owner.GetIndividualNamePlural]闯入其中更加的困难，除非我们拉警戒线隔绝这片区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

It appears that the alien labyrinth on [Target.GetName] is attempting to attract our attention. While it seems surprising that a building would possess any form of consciousness, that is the only way to put it: the labyrinth is now no longer situated in a remote mountain pass, but is now instead located a short distance from one of our main storage areas. From one day to the next, it inexplicably shifted its location, and the warning signs at the entrance have disappeared - in fact, it now looks... inviting.\n\nThis change will make it harder to prevent any [owner.GetIndividualNamePlural] from stumbling into it, unless we make an effort to cordon off the area.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:92`

吸引关注

### English

Attention Seeking

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:97`

## situation.15.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.15.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

这个迷宫似乎对于我们毫不重视的态度闹脾气了。整个结构开始逐渐发生改变，从一幢冷峻的摩天大楼变成了富丽堂皇的宫殿，看上去令人感到很舒适。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

The labyrinth appears to resent our lack of attention. The entire structure has gradually changed in appearance, morphing from a forbidding edifice into what looks remarkably like a pleasure palace.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:98`

## situation.15.desc.base.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.15.desc.base.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]看到这个改变都呆了，现在要阻止人进入这个结构可以说是难上加难。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] are bemused at the changes, and it is becoming ever harder to prevent anyone from entering the structure.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:99`

## situation.15.desc.standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.15.desc.standard&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.15.desc.base$警告危险的标志已经消失了；现在，可爱的[Owner.Species.GetClass][Owner.Species.GetSpawnNamePlural]在入口附近爬来爬去，祈求我们的关注。除此之外，不知怎的，入口里面视线的尽头，似乎显露出的是无边无际的沙滩以及无止尽的明媚阳光。\n\n$situation.15.desc.base.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

$situation.15.desc.base$ Gone is the sign warning of danger; now, adorable [Owner.Species.GetClass] [Owner.Species.GetSpawnNamePlural] crawl around the entrance, begging for attention, and somehow, the insides give the impression of vast beaches and unending sunrises just beyond sight.\n\n$situation.15.desc.base.end$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:100`

## situation.15.desc.human

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.15.desc.human&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.15.desc.base$警告危险的标志已经消失了；现在，可爱的小猫咪在入口附近爬来爬去，一边喵喵叫祈求我们的关注。除此之外，不知怎的，入口里面视线的尽头，似乎显露出的是无边无际的沙滩以及无止尽的明媚阳光。\n\n$situation.15.desc.base.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

$situation.15.desc.base$ Gone is the sign warning of danger; now, adorable kittens crawl around the entrance, mewing for attention, and somehow, the insides give the impression of vast beaches and unending sunrises just beyond sight.\n\n$situation.15.desc.base.end$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:101`

## situation.15.desc.gestalt.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.15.desc.gestalt.base&lang=en)

### 中文

这个迷宫似乎对于我们毫不重视的态度闹脾气了。整个结构开始逐渐发生改变，从一幢冷峻的摩天大楼变成了装饰奢华的存储设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

The labyrinth appears to resent our lack of attention. The entire structure has gradually changed in appearance, morphing from a forbidding edifice into what looks remarkably like a luxurious storage facility.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:102`

## situation.15.desc.gestalt.base.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.15.desc.gestalt.base.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]看到这个改变都呆了，现在要阻止[Root.Owner.GetIndividualNamePlural]进入这个结构可以说是难上加难。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] are bemused at the changes, and it is becoming ever harder to prevent [Root.Owner.GetIndividualNamePlural] from entering the structure.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:103`

## situation.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

禁欲的奖励

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

A Reward for Obstinance

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:108`

## situation.20.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.20.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

迷宫已经不在了。从某一天开始，它就消失得无影无踪了。在原来的地址上，有一张小纸条留给了我们：“尽管我们的最初意图是使用我们的设施研究你们这个位面的生命体，你们禁欲式的拒绝进入也从另一方面给我们上了生动的一课。我们现在有了丰富的经验——它将成为我们研究经历中最有用的成果。作为我们善意的象征，我们在研究实验室中留下了一个微小的时空异常现象。我们希望它对于你们来说足够…有趣。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

The labyrinth is gone. From one day to the next, it simply disappeared. On its site, a message was left for us: "Although it was our intention to study the Beings of your Dimension within our facility, your obstinate refusal to enter despite all the temptations offered has been an education of its own. We are left enriched by the experience - it will prove most useful to our... endeavors. As a token of our goodwill, we leave you a minor space-time anomaly at the site of our laboratory. We hope it will prove... diverting, to you."

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:109`

## situation.25.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.25.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷宫被毁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Labyrinth Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:110`

## situation.25.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.25.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管迷宫展现出非比寻常的特性，事实证明它依然捱不过我们的重武器。当它轰然倒塌时，只有卵石遍地。\n\n不幸的是，我们没法从断壁残垣中榨取什么有用的情报，好在从建材中我们设法回收了一些异星材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

Despite its unusual properties, the labyrinth proved no match for our heavy weaponry. Where it lay, only rubble remains.\n\nUnfortunately, we have not managed to glean much from the wreckage, but some of the exotic elements used in its construction are salvageable.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:111`

## situation.30.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.30.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷宫消失

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

Labyrinth Disappeared

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:112`

## situation.30.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.30.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

迷宫不存在了。从某一天开始，它就凭空消失了。它原先矗立的地方什么也没有留下，只剩下我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]对着原来迷宫在的地方无能狂怒。或许什么都不知道，我们反而更安全一些吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

The labyrinth is gone. From one day to the next, it simply vanished. Nothing remains on the site where it once stood, leaving our [Owner.GetResearchers] baffled as to what it was all about. But perhaps we are safer not knowing.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:113`

## situation.30.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.30.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们摆脱了一个威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

A threat is lifted from us.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:114`

## situation.40.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.40.name&lang=en)

### 中文

探险队报告

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Expedition Report

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:115`

## situation.40.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.40.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们派去调查迷宫的[Owner.GetResearchers]刚一进入，我们同他们的通讯链接就被切断了。我们焦急地等待，但是几天以来没有收到任何消息。随着时间流逝，我们不得不放弃搜寻我们失踪的人。\n\n接着，正当我们放弃一切希望的时候，他们重新冒了出来！然后，这只是带给我们更多的问题：[Owner.GetResearchers]宣称完全不记得他们在迷宫里待过，然后他们的探险记录也被严重篡改。不仅如此，尽管他们只在迷宫里经过了几周，他们看上去却像老了好几岁！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

Our communication link to the [Owner.GetResearchers] we sent into the labyrinth was cut off almost as soon as they entered. Nothing was heard for several days as we waited anxiously. As time passed, we resigned ourselves to the loss of our people.\n\nThen, once all hope was lost, they reemerged! However, this has only raised further questions: the [Owner.GetResearchers] claim to have no memory of their stay within the labyrinth, and their recordings of their expedition are horribly garbled. Furthermore, even though they were in the labyrinth for a matter of weeks, they seem to have aged several years!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:116`

## situation.45.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.45.name&lang=en)

### 中文

持续的困惑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

Continued Perplexity

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:117`

## situation.45.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.45.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对迷宫的进一步探索带来了各种各样的结果，有时是令人不安的那种。有一次，[Owner.GetResearchers]进入后没过几秒就出来了，而且回答不出为什么这么快就返回。而在另一次，他们消失在迷宫中长达数周，但回来后却似乎比去之前更加年轻了，记忆也随之出现了数年的空缺。一些队员返回时容光焕发，而某些队员却是身受重伤。\n\n随着时间推移，入口处的警示不断改变，“进入者自行承担风险”变成了“擅闯者死”，最后则是“警告！警告！不要进入迷宫，否则整颗星球就会灰飞烟灭”。墙壁上毛骨悚然，嵌入着[Owner.GetAdj][Owner.Species.GetRemnantNamePlural]的躯壳，然而最近没有出现不明的失踪事件，因此我们也不知道他们究竟是从哪里来的。\n\n目前尚不清楚更进一步的研究究竟会带来什么，正如[Owner.GetResearchers]说的那样，我们无法证实或者证伪这些警告。然而，这已经使得自愿进入迷宫的人数量骤减。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

Further expeditions into the labyrinth have yielded varying, at times unnerving results. On one occasion, our [Owner.GetResearchers] were in for mere seconds, and could give no answer as to why they turned back so quickly. On another, they were gone for weeks, but came back seemingly younger than they had left, and with year-long gaps in their memories. Some groups returned in a better physical state than they had left; others bore fresh scars of serious injuries.\n\nOver time, the sign at the entrance has changed, too. "Enter at your own risk" became "Danger of death," and finally "WARNING! WARNING! Do not enter the labyrinth. If you do, the planet will explode." In a macabre touch, [Owner.GetAdj] [Owner.Species.GetRemnantNamePlural] adorn the walls - though exactly where they came from is not apparent, since there have been no recent unexplained disappearances.\n\nIt is not clear what further study will bring - as our [Owner.GetResearchers] point out, we have no way to confirm or deny the warning stated on the sign. It is, however, becoming ever harder to find volunteers to enter the labyrinth.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:118`

## situation.45.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.45.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

对迷宫的进一步探索带来了各种各样的结果，有时是令人不安的那种。一次，我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]只进去了几秒钟，而且说不出为什么这么快就回来了。有一次，他们进去了几周，但回来时记忆中的空白长达一年。一些小组返回时的身体状况比他们离开时更好，而有些人则带着严重的新伤痕。\n\n随着时间的推移，入口处的标志也在发生变化。“进入者自行承担风险。”先是变成了“脱离集体的危险”，最后变成了“警告！警告！不要进入迷宫，否则行星会原地爆炸。”墙壁上毛骨悚然，镶嵌着[Owner.GetAdj][Owner.Species.GetRemnantNamePlural]的躯壳，然而最近没有出现不明的失踪事件，因此我们也不知道他们究竟是从哪里来的。\n\n目前尚不清楚更进一步的研究究竟会带来什么，正如[Owner.GetResearchers]说的那样，我们无法证实或者证伪这些警告。然而，这已经使得进入迷宫的[owner.GetIndividualNamePlural]数量骤减。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

Further expeditions into the labyrinth have yielded varying, at times unnerving results. On one occasion, our [Owner.GetResearchers] were in for mere seconds, and could give no answer as to why they turned back so quickly. On another, they were gone for weeks, but came back with year-long gaps in their memories. Some groups returned in a better physical state than they had left; others bore fresh scars of serious injuries.\n\nOver time, the sign at the entrance has changed, too. "Enter at your own risk" became "Danger of disembodiment," and finally "WARNING! WARNING! Do not enter the labyrinth. If you do, the planet will explode." In a macabre touch, [Owner.GetAdj] [Owner.Species.GetRemnantNamePlural] adorn the walls - though exactly where they came from is not apparent, since there have been no recent unexplained disappearances.\n\nIt is not clear what further study will bring - as our [Owner.GetResearchers] point out, we have no way to confirm or deny the warning stated on the sign. It is, however, becoming ever harder to find [owner.GetIndividualNamePlural] to enter the labyrinth.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:119`

## situation.45.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.45.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

对迷宫的进一步探索带来了各种各样的结果，有时是令人不安的那种。一次，我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]只进去了几秒钟，而且无法回馈为什么这么快就回来了。有一次，他们进去了几周，但回来时硬盘存储已经被清空。一些小组返回时的外壳的比他们离开时加崭新，而有些则带着严重的损伤并缺失了一些部件。\n\n随着时间的推移，入口处的标志也在发生变化。“进入者自行承担风险。”先是变成了“拆解危险”，最后变成了“警告！警告！不要进入迷宫，否则行星会原地爆炸。”墙壁上毛骨悚然，镶嵌着[Owner.GetAdj][Owner.Species.GetRemnantNamePlural]的躯壳，然而最近没有出现不明的失踪事件，因此我们也不知道他们究竟是从哪里来的。\n\n目前尚不清楚更进一步的研究究竟会带来什么，正如[Owner.GetResearchers]说的那样，我们无法证实或者证伪这些警告。然而，这已经使得进入迷宫的子个体数量骤减。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

Further expeditions into the labyrinth have yielded varying, at times unnerving results. On one occasion, our [Owner.GetResearchers] were in for mere seconds, and could give no answer as to why they turned back so quickly. On another, they were gone for weeks, and returned with their hard drives wiped. Some groups returned showing less wear and tear on their casing; others sported large dents or missing appendages.\n\nOver time, the sign at the entrance has changed, too. "Enter at your own risk" became "Danger of disassembly," and finally "WARNING! WARNING! Do not enter the labyrinth. If you do, the planet will explode." In a macabre touch, [Owner.GetAdj] [Owner.Species.GetRemnantNamePlural] adorn the walls - though exactly where they came from is not apparent, since there have been no recent unexplained disappearances.\n\nIt is not clear what further study will bring - as our [Owner.GetResearchers] point out, we have no way to confirm or deny the warning stated on the sign. It is, however, becoming ever harder to find drones to enter the labyrinth.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:120`

## situation.50.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.50.name&lang=en)

### 中文

消失

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

A Departure

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:121`

## situation.50.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.50.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那些警告最终没有成真，迷宫的消失没有毁灭[Target.GetName]。相反，迷宫消失得悄无声息——当某天来临，它就已经不在了。但迷宫还是留下了一条信息：“我们感谢你们如此友好地允许我们研究你们这个位面的生命体。事实证明，我们的...努力起到了回报。幸运的是，异次元安全部对你们非常……感兴趣，他们为我们顺利退出提供了资金。作为我们善意的象征，我们在研究实验室中留下了一个微小的时空异常现象。我们希望它对于你们来说足够…有趣。\"

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

Contrary to its warnings, the labyrinth's disappearance did not destroy [Target.GetName]. Instead, its departure was quite unremarkable - from one day to the next, it was simply gone. A message was left behind: "We thank you for so kindly allowing us to study the Beings of your Dimension. It has proven most useful to our... endeavors. Fortunately, you proved sufficiently... fascinating to the Ministry of Extradimensional Security for them to provide us funding for a smooth exit. As a token of our goodwill, we leave you a minor space-time anomaly at the site of our laboratory. We hope it will prove... diverting."

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:122`

## situation.50.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.50.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应当深挖它的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

We shall delve into its secrets.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:123`

## situation.55.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.55.name&lang=en)

### 中文

轰轰烈烈的退场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

A Turbulent Departure

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:124`

## situation.55.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.55.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那些警告最终没有成真，迷宫的消失没有毁灭[Target.GetName]。不过，它可不是消失得毫无痕迹：地震席卷了整个大地，沉睡的火山从睡梦中苏醒。\n\n在一片狼藉中，迷宫还是留下了一条信息：“我们感谢你们如此友好地允许我们研究你们这个位面的生命体。事实证明，我们的……努力获得了回报。对于我们的离开时造成的混乱，我们深表歉意，这是因为我们无法从异次元安全部获得足够的资金用于顺利撤离。作为我们善意的象征，我们在研究实验室中留下了一个微小的时空异常现象。我们希望它对于你们来说足够…有趣。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

Contrary to its warnings, the labyrinth's disappearance did not destroy [Target.GetName]. However, it certainly left its mark: earthquakes ravaged the land, and volcanoes, long-dormant, erupted into life.\n\nAmidst the widespread destruction, a message was left behind: "We thank you for so kindly allowing us to study the Beings of your Dimension. It has proven most useful to our... endeavors. We apologize for the inconvenience our departure causes - we were not able to secure necessary funding from the Ministry of Extradimensional Security for a smooth withdrawal. As a token of our goodwill, we leave you a minor space-time anomaly at the site of our laboratory. We hope it will prove... diverting."

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:125`

## situation.150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Root.GetName]的殖民者汇报了一个奇怪的现象：在他们一个主要殖民定居点外，“雪”开始聚集起来。\n\n好吧，“雪”只是他们用来描述这种物质的代名词。显而易见的是这肯定不是真正的雪，因为[Root.GetName]可是一颗沙漠星球，即使在两极地区温度也极少降到冰点以下。而且，也没有任何人目击到这些东西从天而降。不过，初步的扫描显示这种物质确实主要由冰冻的水构成，至于为什么不会融化仍不清楚。我们的[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]希望能进一步研究它的性质，而另一些人则认为它会带来危险，并希望能把这些雪弄走。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

Our colonists on [Root.GetName] report a strange phenomenon: snow has begun gathering outside of one of their main colonial settlements.\n\nWell, "snow" is the term they have used to describe it, anyway. It would seem implausible that it is real snow, given that [Root.GetName] is a desert planet where temperatures are seldom below freezing, even in the polar regions. Besides, there were no sightings of it actually falling from the sky. Nevertheless, initial readings suggest that the substance does indeed consist mainly of frozen water - though quite why it's not melting is unclear. Our [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] are eager to investigate its properties, but others caution that it may be dangerous and want it removed.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:141`

## situation.155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

散播寒意

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

Spreading Chills

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:142`

## situation.155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Target.GetName]上的“雪”增殖了起来。从起初的一小片突然爆发性增长，覆盖了越来越大面积的沙漠，现在已经到了影响交通和耕种系统的程度。大家越来越担心，如果雪不停止生长，殖民地很有可能会变得难以自持。\n\n我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]在徒劳地寻找这种异象的解释。当从主体上分离开时，这些雪很快就熔化了，只留下了一点点有机残余物。我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]将这些残余物解释为维系这些雪的整体性的原因，通过未知的化学过程，使雪不受外界热量影响。然而，他们也表明这只是目前为止最有可能的猜测，尽管仍在努力，但对这些奇怪的物质目前还知之甚少。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

The snow on [Target.GetName] has been multiplying. The initial small patch has swelled to cover an ever-expanding area of the desert, to the point that it is interfering with our transport and cultivation networks. If it does not stop growing soon, there are growing concerns that the colony will be unable to continue to support itself.\n\nOur [Owner.GetResearchers] on the planet have sought, in vain, to explain the phenomenon. When removed from the main body, the snow quickly melts, leaving behind only some organic matter. Our [Owner.GetResearchers] theorize that this residue is causing the snow to maintain its integrity, sheltering it from heat via an unknown chemical reaction. However, they caution that this is only their best guess, and they have managed to find out very little about the strange compound.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:143`

## situation.160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

移除问题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

Removal Problems

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:148`

## situation.160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些“雪”似乎很抗拒被移除。尽管我们可以从其主体上取下小块使其融化，但这对阻止其势不可挡的增殖似乎收效甚微，而更激进的手段目前为止统统失败了——整个行动过程中围绕着一连串神秘的机械故障。\n\n更糟糕的是，有越来越多的报告称，在受到“雪”影响的范围内有殖民者失踪。这些事件似乎是有所关联的，而这些雪可能是有生命，而且有恶意的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

The snow, it appears, is resistant to being removed. While we are able to remove small parts of it from the main body and melt it in that manner, this has done little to halt its onward march, and more invasive attempts to destroy it have so far failed - a spate of mysterious mechanical failures has surrounded the entire effort.\n\nWhat is worse, there have been increasing reports of colonists disappearing in areas affected by the snow. It seems likely that they are connected and the snow is alive - and malicious.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:149`

## situation.165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被雪淹没

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

Swamped by Snow

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:155`

## situation.165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Target.GetName]上的雪正在以相当危险的速度增殖，现在已经覆盖了行星赤道附近相当不小的一片区域。我们的军事领袖警告道，如果我们想要阻止它的蔓延就需要采取激进手段。然而，另外一些人则认为目前为止这只是个无关痛痒的小麻烦，还不至于说是威胁，而且它也在避免直接占领已殖民的区域。他们认定这东西真的有生命，并认为我们需要寻求与之沟通对话。\n\n不管怎么样，我们可以活动的空间已经越来越狭小了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

The snow on [Target.GetName] is spreading at a quite alarming rate, and now covers a not-insignificant portion of the planet's equatorial areas. Our military leaders warn that drastic action is needed immediately if we are to halt its advance. However, more level heads point out that it has been a nuisance rather than a menace so far, and it has avoided directly occupying inhabited areas. They posit that it is clearly alive, and argue that we should seek to open dialog with it.\n\nEither way, our room for maneuver is rapidly shrinking.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:156`

## situation.170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被雪吞噬

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

Swallowed by the Snow

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:160`

## situation.170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了一些令人担忧的汇报，事关[Target.GetName]的“雪的问题”。似乎有数名殖民者在去“打雪仗”之后失踪了。与他们的联络已经中断了数日，而且我们已经无法找到任何他们的踪迹了，活不见人死不见尸。我们现在已经对当地殖民者通告，不要与这些雪有任何形式的接触，直到我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]找到更多的线索——或者找到那些失踪的人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

We have heard concerning reports regarding the "snow problem" on [Target.GetName]. It appears that several of our colonists have gone missing, having set off to engage in a "snowball fight." Contact was lost with them several days ago, but we have been unable to find any trace of them - living or dead. Colonists are being advised to avoid all contact with the snow, until our [Owner.GetResearchers] can find out more about it - or the lost settlers are found.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:161`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.170.A&lang=en)

### English

## situation.175.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.175.name&lang=en)

### 中文

气候变化

### English

Climate Change

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:165`

## situation.175.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.175.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Target.GetName]上雪的扩散对我们的殖民者而言已经不仅仅是什么新奇小事了，现在它还带了了一些困难。它对生态系统造成了剧烈的影响，许多当地的动植物种群承受了巨大的压力。目前从受影响地区已迁出了大量移民。然而出乎意料的是，有另一些物种在这种新的寒冷环境中欣欣向荣。我们的气候学家敦促对事态进展的关注，并称其为观察正在进行中的非[Owner.GetAdj]操控气候变化的“百年难得一遇”的好机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

The spread of the snow on [Target.GetName] has not just been a source of novelty - and difficulty - for our colonists. It has also caused rapid changes to the ecosystem, with various native species of flora and fauna put under enormous strain. Already, there have been massive migrations away from the affected areas. However, to our surprise, certain other species have thrived in the new, colder conditions. Our climatologists eagerly observe the developments, calling it a "once in a lifetime" opportunity to watch rapid non-[Owner.GetAdj] controlled climate change in action.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:166`

## situation.180.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.180.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的出现给我们带来了智慧。我们通过进食你们的失踪个体学会了新的思考和生存方式。不过你们似乎想毁灭我们。我们最近才刚刚获得了智能，并且决定捍卫它。你们的态度让我们别无选择，只能开战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Your presence has given us intelligence. We have fed off your Lost Ones to learn new ways of thinking, new ways of existing. But you seek to destroy us. We have only recently acquired sapience; we are not yet willing to give it up. Your stance has left us with no choice: this means war.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:167`

## situation.180.A

### 中文

$situation.185.B$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

$situation.185.B$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:168`

## situation.185.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.185.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的出现给我们带来了智慧。我们通过进食你们的失踪个体学会了新的思考和生存方式。不过你们似乎想毁灭我们。我们还不清楚这个存在状态，这种智能到底意味着什么。我们希望可以在这颗星球的角落寻找到属于自己的一席之地，我们可以无拘无束地探索自身。\n\n如果你们不允许我们这么做，那要注意我们并不打算放弃我们的新形态。在必要的情况下我们会保卫自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

Your presence has given us intelligence. We have fed off your Lost Ones to learn new ways of thinking, new ways of existing. We do not yet know what this state of being - this sapience - means. It is our wish to seclude ourselves to a corner of our planet, where we can have free rein to explore what it is to be us.\n\nIf you do not allow us this, be warned that we are not yet willing to give up our new existence. We will defend ourselves, if necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:169`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

We can spare some land.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:170`

## d_living_snow_reserve_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_living_snow_reserve_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一处可以让活雪和平生存并探索自身新的存在状态的保留地。作为回报，我们也可以在这里研究其产生的独特化学过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

A reservation where the Living Snow can live in peace and explore its new state of being. And where, in turn, we can observe the unique chemical reactions it is able to produce.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:175`

## situation.190.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.190.name&lang=en)

殖民地丢失！

### English

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:177`

## situation.190.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.190.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们失去了对[FromFrom.GetName]的控制，它被§Y活雪§!夺走了！在[FromFrom.GetPlanetMoon]的生态系统中已经开始发生非常明显的变化。我们的[This.GetResearchers]提醒，除非我们能尽快夺回[FromFrom.GetPlanetMoon]，否则它的构造变化很快将使我们无法再继续居住。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

We have lost control of [FromFrom.GetName] - which has been seized by the §YLiving Snow§!! Already, significant changes are happening to the [FromFrom.GetPlanetMoon]'s ecosystem. Our [This.GetResearchers] warn that, unless we can retake the [FromFrom.GetPlanetMoon], its makeup will soon be changed beyond our ability to inhabit it.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:178`

## situation.190.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.190.tooltip&lang=en)

§R我们应该尽快夺回行星，否则就来不及了！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

§RWe should retake the planet, before it's too late!§!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:179`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:180`

## situation.195.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.195.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着活雪对[Root.GetName]控制权的巩固，[Root.GetPlanetMoon]也变得越来越冷，它的大气条件在越来越快地发生改变。可以说，这颗星球现在已经不可居住了。目前暂不清楚我们是否能够将其恢复到之前的状态……不过我们的[saved_owner.GetResearchers]确信，在这些雪发现了自身的新存在形态，并通过自身独特的化学反应将整个[Root.GetName]变得天翻地覆之后，我们可以通过研究这些雪的独特行为学会许多有趣的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

With the Living Snow consolidating its control over [Root.GetName], the [Root.GetPlanetMoon] has become ever colder, its atmospheric conditions changing at an ever-increasing rate. Now, the world can no longer be considered inhabitable. It is unclear whether we will ever be able to restore it to its former state... but our [saved_owner.GetResearchers] assure us that much of interest can be learned by observing the antics of the snow as it discovers its new state of being - and its unique chemical reactions which have wrought such change upon [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:181`

## situation_geomagnetic_storm_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_geomagnetic_storm_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场恒星活动的激增正在削弱§Y[Target.GetName]§!的磁层，将我们的机械暴露在危险量级的电离辐射中。投资辐射抵抗技术可以保护我们的子个体在风暴结束之前免受损伤。\n§H该局势会在§Y[Target.storm_duration]§!月之内结束。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

An increase in solar activity is weakening §Y[Target.GetName]§!'s magnetosphere, exposing our machines to dangerous levels of ionizing radiation. Invest in radiation hardening techniques to shield our drones against damage until the storm subsides.\n§HThe situation will end in §Y[Target.storm_duration]§! month(s).§!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:191`

## situation_geomagnetic_storm_monthly_change_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_geomagnetic_storm_monthly_change_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

该§Y局势§!的进度取决于我们所选的§Y解决方案§!。保护子个体以避免积累性辐射损伤，防止走向负面结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

The progress of this §YSituation§! is dependent on the §YApproach§! we pick. Shield the drones to avoid accumulating radiation damage and prevent a negative outcome.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:192`

## situation_geomagnetic_storm_outcome

## radiation_hardened_components_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aradiation_hardened_components_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的帝国已经完全掌握保护机械部件不受宇宙射线损伤的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

## situation.200.name

### 中文

恒星耀斑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Solar Flare

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:231`

## situation.200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

警告：在§Y[This.GetStarName]§!上检测到了恒星耀斑爆发。恒星风冲击波正在削弱§Y[This.GetName]§!的磁层，使我们的无人机暴露在越来越高水平的电离辐射中。由此产生的软件损坏风险蔓延到我们的核心智能，可能对我们整个帝国造成毁灭性后果。\n\n优先事项：使用防辐射技术以保护无人机部件，以对抗辐射。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

WARNING: eruption of a solar flare detected on §Y[This.GetStarName]§!. The solar wind shockwave is weakening §Y[This.GetName]§!'s magnetosphere, exposing our drones to increased levels of ionizing radiation. The resulting software damage risks spreading to our Core intelligence, with potentially devastating consequences for our whole empire.\n\nPriority: employ radiation-hardening techniques to shield drone components, making them impervious to radiation.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:232`

## situation.202.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.202.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

状态更新：数据确认§Y[root.GetStarName]§!已经进入了一个剧烈活动的阶段，其特点是恒星黑子增加、日冕物质抛射以及磁爆发。我们的[Root.owner.GetScientistPlural]估计这颗恒星会在§H五年§!内恢复到正常的活动水平。\n\n建议行动计划：继续生产防辐射部件以保护我们的无人机在风暴结束之前不受损伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

Status update: data confirms §Y[root.GetStarName]§! has entered a phase of intense activity, characterized by increased solar spots, coronal mass ejections, and magnetic outbursts. Our [Root.owner.GetScientistPlural] estimate the star will return to a normal level of activity within §Hfive years§!.\n\nSuggested plan of action: continue to produce radiation-hardened components to shield our drones from radiation damage until the storm subsides.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:243`

## situation.203.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.203.name&lang=en)

### 中文

看护者的看护者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

Who Watches The Watchers?

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:246`

## situation.203.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.203.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

警告：活体陈设发生异常行为。虽然大多数人对他们的机械仆从能力下降表示烦恼，但一小部分现在正在帮助他们的仆从执行日常任务，并以“怜悯”和“同情”作为他们行动的动机。他们现在呼吁建立一个“幼儿园”来照顾他们功能故障的看护者。\n\n先导性分析表明，沉迷于活体陈设将对他们的自我价值感产生积极影响，但也可能使他们面临无法预料的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

Warning: bio-trophies are displaying anomalous behavior. While most expressed annoyance at the reduced capacities of their mechanical servitors, a small percentage are now helping their servitors execute routine tasks, citing "pity" and "sympathy" as motivation for their actions. They're now calling for the institution of a "care home" to watch over their malfunctioning caretakers.\n\nPre-emptive analysis suggests indulging the bio-trophies will have a positive impact on their sense of self-worth, but may also expose them to unforeseen dangers.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:247`

## situation.213.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.213.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

## situation.215.name

### English

## situation.215.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.215.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

库存报告：我们拥有的物品数量与我们注册表中的预期数量不符。可能原因：内存库损坏。重制我们的注册表并将其移动到更稳定的机器上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

Inventory Report: the number of goods in our possession does not match the expected amount in our registry. Suspected cause: corrupted Memory Banks. Reconciling our registry and moving it to more stable machines.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:298`

## situation.215.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.215.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y故障：我们库存的实际资源数量低于登记数量。原因：数学太难了。重计数/计算/检查资源。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

§YProblem: number of resources in our stockpile lower than inventory numbers. Cause: math is hard. Recounting / calculating / checking resources.§!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:300`

## situation.216.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.216.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

状态报告：得益于$job_machine_nurse_plural$对我们的库存进行了双重检查，我们能够检测并修复由损坏的内存库引起的一些错误计算。结果，很明显我们库存中的总资源高于最初的估计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

Status report: thanks to the $job_machine_nurse_plural$ double-checking our inventory, we were able to detect and fix a number of miscalculations caused by corrupted memory banks. As a result, it has become evident that the total resources in our stockpile is higher than originally estimated.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:310`

## situation.216.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.216.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y故障：我们库存中的资源数量与库存中的预期数量不匹配。原因：数学太难了。但是$job_machine_nurse_plural$帮忙做了作业。找到/发现/重新获得丢失的资源。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

§YProblem: number of resources in our stockpile does not match the amount expected in inventory. Cause: math is hard. But $job_machine_nurse_plural$ helped with homework. Found / discovered / regained missing resources.§!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:312`

## situation.250.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.250.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

错误：无法确定一组无人机位置。该小组离开工作设施进行强制性维护，却在前往存储区的路上消失了。调查工作正在进行中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Error: unable to determine the location of a squad of drones. The group left the work facility for their mandatory maintenance round only to disappear on their way to the housing district. Investigative efforts are ongoing.

## situation.251.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.251.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

情况更新：失踪无人机小队的位置已确认。这些机器被发现站在田野上，尽管已经启动，但他们一动不动没有任何反应。初步分析证实，他们的内存驱动器已被磁爆擦除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Update: location of missing drone squad confirmed. The machines were found standing on a field, unmoving and unresponsive despite being switched on. Preliminary analysis confirms their memory drives were wiped by a magnetic burst.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:332`

## situation.252.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.252.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

状态报告：丢失的无人机已成功重新激活并重新融入我们的工作队伍。\n\n然而由于持续存在的硬件损坏，他们的功能表现明显下降。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

Status report: the missing drones have been successfully reactivated and reintegrated within our workforce.\n\nHowever, due to lingering hardware damage, their performance functionality has noticeably decreased.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:349`

## situation.291.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.291.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§R[Root.Owner.GetName]BIOS版本3.3.3\n[Root.owner.GetSpeciesName]公司版权所有。§!\n§Y内存检测：43053475903PB 完成\n\n检测思潮及国民理念……完成\n检测库存资料……完成\n检测殖民地信息……§! §R故障§!\n\n重装完成。\n\n§Y[Target.GetName]§!上故障无人机的认知链接已被切断以避免软件损坏。§Y[Target.GetName]§!上检测不到运行中的的无人机。殖民地发展停止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

§R[Root.Owner.GetName] BIOS v3.3.3\nCopyright date, [Root.owner.GetSpeciesName] Inc. §!\n§YMemory test: 43053475903PB OK\n\nDetecting ethics and civics... OK\nDetecting stockpile data... OK\nDetecting colonies data...§!  §RERROR§!\n\nReboot complete.\n\nThe cognitive link to malfunctioning drones on §Y[Target.GetName]§! has been severed to contain software damage. No working drones on §Y[Target.GetName]§! detected. Colony development arrested.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:376`

## situation.292.name

### English

After The Storm

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:379`

## situation.292.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.292.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

状态报告：§Y[Target.GetName]§!上的辐射水平已经趋于稳定。§Y[Target.GetStarName]§!已恢复到正常的恒星活动水平。地磁风暴已经结束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

Status report: radiation levels on §Y[Target.GetName]§! are now stable. §Y[Target.GetStarName]§! has returned to normal levels of solar activity. The geomagnetic storm has ended.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:380`

## situation.292.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.292.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.292.desc.base$\n\n最终报告：这一挑战性的局势给予我们一个完善防辐射技术的机遇，提升了我们无人机在极端条件下生存的能力。感激这一结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

$situation.292.desc.base$\n\nFinal report: the challenging situation gave us an opportunity to perfect our radiation-hardening techniques, increasing our drones' capabilities to survive in extreme conditions. We appreciate this outcome.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:381`

## situation.293.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.293.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.292.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

$situation.292.name$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:384`

## situation.293.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.293.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.292.desc.base$\n\n最终报告：我们疲于应对风暴，最后还是没能保护我们的无人机不受辐射损伤。尽管我们从挑战中生存下来，但我们也找到了提升的空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

$situation.292.desc.base$\n\nFinal report: we struggled to deal with the storm, and failed to protect our drones from radiation damage. While we were able to survive the challenge, we see room for improvement.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:385`

## situation.294.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.294.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.292.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

$situation.292.name$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:388`

## situation.294.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.294.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.292.desc.base$\n\n最终报告：我们仍在遭受辐射损伤的后续影响。还需要一定时间恢复到理想的认知状态。令人不满的结果。我们在未来必须多家准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

$situation.292.desc.base$\n\nFinal report: we are still suffering from long-lasting consequences of radiation damage. Returning to an optimal cognitive state will require time. Unsatisfying outcome. We must be better prepared for the future.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:389`

## situation_gravity_well_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_gravity_well_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Target.GetName]§!离开了自身的稳定轨道，呈螺旋轨迹进入恒星系的黑洞。尽管这一事件给予我们一个绝无仅有的攫取大量£sr_dark_matter£§H暗物质§!和£physics£§H物理学研究§!的契机，如果我们不迅速行动抵消黑洞的拖拽引力，这座空间站很快就会被黑洞吞没。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

The §Y[From.Target.GetName]§! has left its stable orbit and is now spiraling into the system's black hole. While the incident offers us a never-before-seen opportunity to harvest £sr_dark_matter£§HDark Matter§! and £physics£ §HPhysics Research§!, if we don't act fast to counteract the gravitational pull of the black hole, the station will soon be swallowed whole.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:401`

## situation_gravity_well_monthly_change_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_gravity_well_monthly_change_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

这个§Y局势§!的进度同时取决于我们所选的§Y解决方案§!和§Y局势§!当前所处的§Y阶段§!。§Y[Target.GetName]§!越接近黑洞，它就越难逃离毁灭的命硬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

The progress of this §YSituation§! is dependent on both the §YApproach§! we pick and the current §YStage§! the §YSituation§! is at. As §Y[Target.GetName]§! nears the black hole, it will become ever more difficult for it to escape its fate.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:402`

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:424`

超越意识

## gestalt_black_hole_good_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agestalt_black_hole_good_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们[Owner.GetBrainName]的一个碎片现在停留在一个黑洞的事件视界上，在这里一秒钟膨胀成了永世。时间差异有了自己的用处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

A fragment of our [Owner.GetBrainName] is lodged in the event horizon of a black hole, where a single second stretches into an eon. The time difference has its uses.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:427`

## gestalt_black_hole_bad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agestalt_black_hole_bad&lang=en)

断裂意识

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

Disjointed Consciousness

## gestalt_black_hole_bad_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agestalt_black_hole_bad_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们[Owner.GetBrainName]的一个碎块已经永远地固锁于黑洞的事件视界以内，其中的每一秒都延长到了数个纪元。时间不同步在刺痛着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

A fragment of our [Owner.GetBrainName] is stuck in the event horizon of a black hole, where seconds stretch out into eons. The time difference is jarring.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:429`

## situation.501.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.501.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自由落体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Free Fall

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:431`

## situation.501.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.501.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于与太空垃圾发生意外碰撞，§Y[gravity_well_station.GetName]§!已经偏离了最佳稳定运转轨道，现在正缓缓落入最近的黑洞。这一事故使得整个空间站陷入了危险，但同时也是一个绝佳的研究黑洞、虹吸大量§H暗物质§!的契机。\n\n应急推进器已经就绪准备修正空间站的轨道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Due to an unexpected collision with a chunk of space debris, the §Y[gravity_well_station.GetName]§! has deviated from its innermost stable circular orbit and is now slowly closing the distance towards the nearby black hole. While the accident puts the whole station at risk, it is also an incredible opportunity to study the black hole and siphon huge quantities of §HDark Matter§!.\n\nEmergency thrusters have been deployed to correct the station's orbit.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:432`

## situation.501.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.501.a&lang=en)

### 中文

赶紧远离吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

Get away from there!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:433`

## situation.501.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.501.b&lang=en)

### 中文

围绕黑洞稳定空间站轨道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Stabilize the station's orbit around the black hole.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:434`

## situation.501.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.501.c&lang=en)

### 中文

尽可能接近黑洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Get as close as possible to the black hole.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:435`

## situation.510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深渊致意

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

Hello Darkness

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:438`

## situation.510.spirit.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.510.spirit.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

神迹出现了！受到宗教狂热的驱使，§Y[Target.GetName]§!上虔诚的[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]已夺取了空间站的控制权。据他们称，他们获得了启示，现在打算径直前往黑洞中心，履行一桩自我毁灭的精神使命。\n\n武装人员目前正在试图夺回空间站的控制权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

An epiphany! Driven by religious fervor, pious [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] aboard the §Y[Target.GetName]§! have seized control of the station. They claim to have received a vision, and are now heading straight to the center of the black hole in a spiritual quest for self-annihilation.\n\nArmed personnel are currently fighting to regain control of the station.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:439`

## situation.510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[Target.GetName]§!上的[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]被狂热冲昏了头脑，他夺取了空间站的控制权，并将其慢慢拐向黑洞——这番行为显然是出于近乎病态的科学好奇心、严重的鲁莽和对自己生命的彻底漠视。\n\n武装人员目前正在试图夺回空间站的控制权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

Overenthusiastic [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] aboard the §Y[Target.GetName]§! have seized control of the station and are now steering it towards the black hole - a move doubtlessly driven by insatiable scientific curiosity, an unhealthy amount of recklessness, and utter disregard for their own self-preservation.\n\nArmed personnel are currently fighting to regain control of the station.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:440`

## situation.510.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.510.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

局势解决方案已锁定为“前进”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

Situation approach locked to Advance.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:441`

## situation.511.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.511.name&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗之怖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

Fear of the Dark

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:443`

## situation.511.black.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.511.black.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“我们已经见过舰船被事件视界捕获后会发生什么了！我不想摊上这么个死法！”\n\n在咆哮出他们的抗命之词后，我们的一位[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]夺取了空间站的控制权，并且将其缓缓驶离黑洞。\n\n武装人员目前正在试图夺回空间站的控制权。此外，涉及到这一事故的相关物理知识将会从课本当中删除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

'We've seen what happens to ships caught in the event horizon! I don't intend to die like that!'\n\nAfter screaming their defiance, one of our [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] seized control of the station and is now steering it away from the black hole.\n\nArmed personnel are currently fighting to regain control of the station, and the incident referenced is being expunged from our physics textbooks.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:444`

## situation.511.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.511.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

惊慌的[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]现在控制住了空间站并将其缓缓驶离黑洞。\n\n武装人员目前正在试图夺回空间站的控制权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Terrified [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] have seized control of the station and are now steering it away from the black hole.\n\nArmed personnel are currently fighting to regain control of the station.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:445`

## situation.511.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.511.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

局势解决方案已锁定为“撤退”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

Situation approach locked to Retreat.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:446`

## situation.513.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.513.name&lang=en)

### 中文

夺回控制

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

Regaining Control

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:448`

## situation.513.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.513.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过了紧张的对峙，哗变人员最终被制服了，武装人员重新夺回了§Y[Target.GetName]§!的控制权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

After a tense confrontation, the mutineers have been dealt with, and armed personnel have regained full control of §Y[Target.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:449`

## situation.513.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.513.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

局势解决方案现在可以手动变更了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

Situation approach can again be manually changed.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:450`

## situation.520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

比预想更近

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Closer Than We Thought

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:452`

## situation.520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在接近黑洞的过程中，时空以出乎意料的方式发生了弯曲，而§Y[Target.GetName]§!的仪器也在艰难运转。经过对船上的仪器进行细致的重校准后，我们才发现这座空间站比我们之前预料的更加接近黑洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

When approaching a black hole, spacetime bends in unpredictable ways, and §Y[Target.GetName]§!'s instruments are struggling to keep up. After a careful recalibration of the onboard instruments, the station was shown to be much closer to the black hole than we previously thought.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:453`

## situation.530.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.530.name&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Dark Discoveries

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:455`

## situation.530.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.530.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Target.GetName]§!与黑洞的超近距离使其[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]能获取海量的数据，大大推动了我们对物理学最基本定律的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

§Y[Target.GetName]§!'s proximity to the black hole allowed its [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] to gather a vast amount of data, furthering our understanding of the most fundamental laws of physics.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:456`

## situation.540.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.540.name&lang=en)

### 中文

彭罗斯过程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

The Penrose Process

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:458`

## situation.540.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.540.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的一位[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]提出了一种拯救§Y[Target.GetName]§!避免其毁灭的绝妙方案：通过拆解空间站的外部模块和设备并将它们丢入黑洞，这座空间站应该会获得足够的动量以逃逸黑洞的引力束缚。注意，只是说“应该”，这一决策背后的理论从未得到实验的验证。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

One of our [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] has devised a clever plan to save §Y[Target.GetName]§! from its fate: by stripping away any extraneous modules and equipment and hurling them into the black hole, the station should gain enough momentum to escape the black hole's gravitational pull. In this case, 'should' is the operative word: the theory behind this decision has never before been tested.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:459`

## situation.540.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.540.a&lang=en)

### 中文

值得一试。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

It's worth a try.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:460`

## situation.540.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.540.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

拆除§Y[Target.GetName]§!上的所有建筑，以尝试减少§Y局势进展§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

Removes all Buildings from §Y[Target.GetName]§! in an attempt to decrease §Ysituation progress§!.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:461`

## situation.540.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.540.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这是在无谓地浪费资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

A needless waste of resources.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:462`

## situation.541.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.541.name&lang=en)

### 中文

彭罗斯结果

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

The Penrose Result

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:464`

## situation.541.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.541.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Target.GetName]§!上的人员拆除了空间站的模块之后，将它们全部扔向黑洞。空间站和模块分离时产生的动量成功推动了空间站远离黑洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

After stripping the station of its modules, the personnel aboard §Y[Target.GetName]§! Jettisoned them into the black hole. The momentum caused by the launch has successfully propelled the station away from the singularity.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:465`

## situation.542.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.542.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.541.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

$situation.541.name$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:467`

## situation.542.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.542.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.541.desc$\n\n尽管拆卸抛弃空间站模块的决定为我们争取了更多时间，但是其产生的效果却远远小于我们的预期。这座空间站还是没能达到逃逸速度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

$situation.541.desc$\n\nWhile the decision to jettison the station modules has gained us more time, the results were less effected than expected. The station has not reached escape velocity.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:468`

## situation.580.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.580.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不归点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

Point Of No Return

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:470`

## situation.580.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.580.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Target.GetName]§!已经太过靠近黑洞，它的推进器无法补偿剧烈增大的引力。\n\n我们已经注定失去这座空间站了。如今我们能做的事只有延缓它终结的到来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

§Y[Target.GetName]§! got too close to the black hole, and its thrusters are unable to oppose the increased gravitational pull.\n\nThe station is lost. All we can do now is delay the inevitable.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:471`

## situation.590.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.590.name&lang=en)

### 中文

事件视界

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Event Horizon

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:474`

## situation.590.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.590.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Target.GetName]§!已经越过了黑洞的事件视界。由于时间膨胀效应，空间站的残影将会在它本身消失很长时间以后仍然留存，在我们的传感器数据上永远刻下它的痕迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

§Y[Target.GetName]§! is crossing the black hole's event horizon. Due to the effects of time dilatation, its afterimage will persist long after the station itself is gone, a memory forever etched in our sensor data.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:475`

## situation.590.desc.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.590.desc.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.590.desc.base$\n\n即使光线也无法穿过黑洞的内轨道，空间站及其乘员的最后时刻将永远未知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

$situation.590.desc.base$\n\nNot even light can cross the inner orbit of a black hole: the final moments of the station and its personnel will forever remain unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:476`

## situation.590.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.590.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.590.desc.base$\n\n除非我们迅速切断与空间站内[owner.GetIndividualNamePlural]的所有联系，否则对所有已知物理定律崩溃的直接可能会不可挽回地损害我们的集体意识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

$situation.590.desc.base$\n\nUnless we quickly sever all connection with the [owner.GetIndividualNamePlural] inside the station, the firsthand experience of the breakdown of all known laws of physics may irreparably damage our shared consciousness.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:477`

## situation.590.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.590.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

警告：§Y[Target.GetName]§!已非常接近黑洞的事件视界。重力吸引：无法逃脱。时间膨胀：剧增。建议执行行动：空间站内残存子个体自毁，以避免直接遭受已知物理定律的崩溃。\n\n§R严重警告：§!若继续维持神经链接，我们的集体意识将遭受无法修复的损伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

Warning: §Y[Target.GetName]§! dangerously close to event horizon. Gravity pull: inescapable. Time dilatation: increasing. Recommended course of action: self-termination of all drones residing inside the station to avoid the collective experience of the breakdown of all known laws of physics.\n\n§RSevere warning:§! chance of irreparable damage to our collective consciousness if the neural link is maintained.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:478`

## situation.590.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.590.a&lang=en)

子个体自毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.590.b&lang=en)

我们必须见证坠落。

### English

We must witness the fall.

## situation.591.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.591.name&lang=en)

### 中文

混乱的时间

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

Time Out Of Joint

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:483`

## situation.591.base.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.591.base.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

意识膨胀。感知减慢如虫蠕。每个姿态都是一个无限的事件序列。我们正在见证无限——不，沐浴在其中。\n就好像时间本身在屏住呼吸。\n\n由于黑洞扭曲了时空织构，我们意识中的一部分凝固在永远的自由落体中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

Thoughts dilate. Perception slows to a crawl. Every gesture is an infinite sequence of events. We are witnessing infinity - no, bathing in it.\nIt's as if time itself is holding its breath.\n\nAs the black hole distorts the fabric of spacetime, a fragment of our consciousness is now stuck in eternal freefall.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:486`

## situation.591.base.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.591.base.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

状态报告：由于与黑洞的距离越来越近，我们在空间站内的无人机正在经历极端的时间膨胀。处理器速度降低。机械速度下降。内部日期系统溢出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Status report: our drones inside the stations are experiencing extreme time dilation due to increased proximity with the black hole. Processor speed decreased. Mechanical speed decreased. Internal date systems overflowing.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:487`

## situation.591.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.591.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.591.base.hive$\n\n我们被囚禁在黑洞中的一部分感知到的一日事实上外部将会过去数个世纪。我们可以利用这点。我们通过切换自身意识，可以通过一个全新的视角分析自己的整个历史，获得新的思维模式。\n\n进这一次，分离使我们强大。从今往后，我们既是自己故事的主角，同时也是读者——我们将为自己书写一个壮丽的结局。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

$situation.591.base.hive$\n\nCenturies will pass while the fragment of ourself stuck within the black hole-self thinks for a day. We can exploit this. By shifting our self, we can now analyze our entire history from a new perspective. In this way, new patterns emerge.\n\nFor once, separation has made us stronger. From now on, we are both protagonists and readers of our own story - and we'll write ourselves a magnificent ending.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:489`

## situation.591.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.591.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.591.base.machine$\n\n结论：该局势可以被利用。该空间站中的无人机可以一日的感知读过外部数个世纪。通过将感知能力重定向至此，我们可以对我们整个帝国的历史获得全新的观察视角。所有不良的处理模式都可以被修正。我们有能力优化自己的历史。\n\n观测：仅此一次，分离使我们强大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

$situation.591.base.machine$\n\nConclusion: this situation can be exploited. Centuries will pass while the drones on the station experience a single day. By redirecting cognitive power to them, we can gain a new perspective on the history of our empire. Any harmful patterns that emerge can be corrected. We can optimize our own future.\n\nObservation: for once, separation has made us stronger.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:490`

## situation.592.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.592.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.591.base.hive$\n\n这感觉太痛苦了。我们的整个意识困于恒久的、永远落后一步的既视感中。正在尝试切断与[owner.GetIndividualNamePlural]的连接，但该过程需要无限长的时间才能生效。\n\n我们学到了不少东西，但是代价是什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

$situation.591.base.hive$\n\nIt's excruciating. A perennial sense of deja-vu, of forever being a step behind. We are trying to sever connection with the [owner.GetIndividualNamePlural], but it will take an infinite amount of time for the process to take effect.\n\nWe have learned much. But at what cost?

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:494`

## situation.592.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.592.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.591.base.machine$\n\n状况报告：遭受认知创伤。无法应对黑洞内时间膨胀。\n尝试切断与无人机链接。预计完成时间：43504967826056783年。\n\n结论：维持链接的决定具有一定启发性，但同时极具破坏性。在任何情况下均不可再重蹈覆辙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

$situation.591.base.machine$\n\nStatus report: suffering cognitive trauma. Unable to cope with the time dilatation within the black hole.\nAttempting to sever connection with the drones. Estimated completion date: 43504967826056783 years.\n\nConclusion: our decision to maintain connection was instructive but ultimately harmful. Do not repeat under any circumstance.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:495`

## situation.596.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.596.name&lang=en)

### 中文

来自黑暗的归还

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

Back From Black

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:498`

## situation.596.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.596.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Target.GetName]§!逃离了黑洞的引力束缚，回归了围绕这颗坍缩恒星的稳定轨道。\n\n在这一危险的过程中获得的数据可以让我们的[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]忙活上好几年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

§Y[Target.GetName]§! escaped the gravitational pull of the black hole, returning to a stable orbit around the collapsed star.\n\nThe data we gathered during this dangerous situation will keep our [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] busy for years.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:499`

## situation.597.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.597.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Target.GetName]§!逃离了黑洞的引力束缚，回归了围绕这颗坍缩恒星的稳定轨道。\n\n尽管许多人说这场经历简直“令人极度痛苦”，但空间站上的一位[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]则受到这次历险的启发，现在热切期望与其他的科学家分享新的洞见，就是也许有点太过热切了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

§Y[Target.GetName]§! escaped the gravitational pull of the black hole, returning to a stable orbit around the collapsed star.\n\n Through many have described the experience as 'utterly traumatizing', one of the [Root.Owner.GetResearchers] aboard the station was inspired by the ordeal, and is now eager - almost too eager - to share their new expertise with the rest of our scientific team.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:501`

## situation_deficit_desc_base

### 中文

## situation_deficit_monthly_change_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_deficit_monthly_change_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的资源缺口越大，该§Y局势§!严重性升级得就越快。\n而资源收支正平衡将会快速降低该§Y局势§!的严重性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

The larger our resource shortfall, the faster this §YSituation§! will escalate in severity.\nHaving a positive monthly resource balance will rapidly reduce this §YSituation§!'s severity.
