## deficit.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的能量币赤字已经失控！[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]要求我们采取有效措施控制事态，否则将会在数月之后大幅削减开支。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

Our energy deficit is out of control! [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] demands that we take drastic action to take hold of the situation, failing which severe cutbacks will be necessary in a matter of months.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:606`

## deficit.100.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.100.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的能量币赤字已经失控！我们的资源分配子个体面对我们补给线健康度的读数时愈发地焦虑不安了。它们预计，如果无法采取有效行动，在数月之后必须要大幅削减开支。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Our energy deficit is out of control! Our resource distribution drones are increasingly agitated at the readings they measure on the health of our supply lines. They forecast that drastic cutbacks will be necessary in a matter of months unless significant action is taken.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:607`

## deficit.100.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.100.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以从支持者那里借一点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

We can borrow from our supporters.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:608`

## deficit.100.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.100.B&lang=en)

### 中文

从[looted_planet.GetName]重分配资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

Reallocate resources from [looted_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:609`

## deficit.100.C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.100.C&lang=en)

### 中文

一切尽在掌握。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Everything is going according to plan.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:610`

## BANKRUPTCY

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABANKRUPTCY&lang=en)

### 中文

破产了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

Bankruptcy!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:611`

## deficit.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能避免破产。经历了数月的能量币短缺之后，我们别无选择，只能拖欠债务并大幅削减开支。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

We have failed to stave off bankruptcy. Following months of energy shortages that have become terminal, we have had no choice but to default on our debts and make sharp cutbacks to our expenditures.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:612`

## deficit.110.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.110.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能平衡收支。经历了数月的能量币短缺之后，我们别无选择，只能对我们的资源分发系统强制执行大规模重组。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

We have failed to rebalance our expenditures. Following months of energy shortages that have become terminal, we have had no choice but to enforce large-scale reallocations to our resource distribution systems.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:613`

## end_deficit_effect_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aend_deficit_effect_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§R所有已升级建筑全部降级。\n解散所有进攻性陆军。\n解散50%军用舰队。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

§RAll upgraded buildings are downgraded.\nAll offensive armies are disbanded.\n50% of military ships are disbanded.§!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:614`

## end_other_deficit_situations_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aend_other_deficit_situations_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

作为我们帝国改组过程的一部分，其他所有赤字局势均已结束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

All other Deficit Situations are ended as part of our Empire's restructuring.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:615`

## deficit.125.name

$deficit.100.name$

### English

$deficit.100.name$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:617`

## deficit.125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们帝国的矿物短缺在最近数月来愈发严峻。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]对前景的预期从担忧转为愤怒，这表明我们很快就会用尽偿还债务的资源。他们预测，除非采取重大行动，否则在数月之后必须要大幅削减开支。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Our empire's mineral shortages have become increasingly severe in recent months. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] has progressed from concerned to irate at the current forecasts, which suggest that we will soon run out of resources to pay off our debts. They predict that drastic cutbacks will be necessary in a matter of months unless significant action is taken.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:618`

## deficit.125.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.125.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们帝国的矿物短缺在最近数月来愈发严峻。我们的资源分配子个体已经竭尽全力，仍然无法保证我们的后勤系统不崩溃。他们预测，除非采取重大行动，否则在数月之后必须要大幅削减开支。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

Our empire's mineral shortages have become increasingly severe in recent months. Our resource allocation drones are stretched to the limit, and are unable to guarantee against a breakdown of our logistics systems. They forecast that drastic cutbacks will be necessary in a matter of months unless significant action is taken.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:619`

## deficit.130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能避免破产。经历了数月的矿物极度短缺之后，我们别无选择，只能拖欠债务并大幅削减开支。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

We have failed to stave off bankruptcy. Following months of mineral shortages that have become terminal, we have had no choice but to default on our debts and make sharp cutbacks to our expenditures.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:620`

## deficit.130.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.130.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能平衡收支。经历了数月的矿物短缺之后，我们别无选择，只能对我们的资源分发系统强制执行大规模重组。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

We have failed to rebalance our expenditures. Following months of mineral shortages that have become terminal, we have had no choice but to enforce large-scale reallocations to our resource distribution systems.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:621`

## deficit.140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

严峻食物短缺

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Acute Food Shortages

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:623`

## deficit.140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们帝国面临的食物短缺问题正变得越来越严重。显然我们必须采取果断行动，否则短缺将在几个月内达到极限。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

The food shortages befalling our empire are becoming increasingly problematic. It is clear that we will have to take decisive action, otherwise the shortages will become terminal within a matter of months.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:624`

## deficit.140.B.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.140.B.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

关闭全部的生物反应堆。我们就不再需要支出相应的维护费了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

All active Bio-Reactors are disabled. We will no longer have to pay their upkeep.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:628`

## deficit.145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的食物债务已经无可救药。大幅削减将会强制执行，我们将别无选择接受破产并重组我们的契约。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Our food debt has become terminal. Sharp cutbacks will be enforced, as we have no choice but to accept bankruptcy and default on our obligations.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:629`

## deficit.145.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.145.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的食物耗尽了。我们无法再为子个体或其他需要食物以维持运转的实体提供给养。大幅削减将会强制执行，我们别无选择，只能对我们的资源分发系统强制执行大规模重组。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Our food has run out. We simply cannot provide any sustenance to the drones and other entities that require it to function. Sharp cutbacks will be enforced, as we have no choice but to make large-scale reallocations to our resource distribution system.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:630`

## deficit.145.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.145.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

我们或许最珍视之物——自然资源的平衡——已经失守。我们的给养储备已经耗尽，导致整个食物链陷入困境。为了确保最大的生存机会，我们现在必须采取严厉的配给制和大规模的资源重新分配。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

That which we perhaps hold most precious - the balance of our natural resources - has failed. Our feed stocks have run out, causing distress throughout the food web. We must now resort to harsh rationing and large-scale reallocation of resources in order to ensure the greatest chance for survival.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:631`

## deficit.155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

消费主义受挫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

Consumerism Thwarted

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:633`

## deficit.155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

消费品的短缺导致[Root.Owner.GetName]境内生活水平的严重下滑。不满的声音日益显现，采取紧急行动的呼吁越来越广泛，很明显，供应的完全崩溃只有数月之遥了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

The shortages of consumer goods have led to a precipitous drop in living standards within the [Root.Owner.GetName]. Voices of discontent are growing ever stronger, and urgent calls to action are increasingly widespread as it becomes clear that a total breakdown in supplies is only months away.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:634`

## deficit.155.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.155.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

消费品的短缺导致了[Root.Owner.GetName]内非格式塔生物的生活水平大幅下跌。不满的声音日益显现，采取紧急行动的呼吁越来越广泛，很明显，供应的完全崩溃只有数月之遥了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Shortages of consumer goods have led to a precipitous drop in living standards for the individualistic life forms living within the [Root.Owner.GetName]. Voices of discontent are growing ever stronger, as our logistics drones warn that a total breakdown in supplies is only months away.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:635`

## deficit.155.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.155.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们得勒紧裤腰带了，制定配给制度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Hard times are upon us. Institute rationing.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:636`

## deficit.160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的消费品短缺已经严重到没有任何切合实际的办法能够弥补。大幅削减将会强制执行，我们将别无选择接受破产并重组我们的契约。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Consumer goods shortages have increased to the point that there is no realistic way our deficits can be recouped. Sharp cutbacks will be enforced, as we have no choice but to accept bankruptcy and default on our obligations.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:637`

## deficit.170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$deficit.100.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

$deficit.100.name$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:639`

## deficit.170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]现在愈发关心我们帝国发生的合金短缺。如果不能尽快弥补上我们的支出，我们的工业部门将会收到广泛的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] is getting increasingly concerned about the alloy shortage befalling our empire. Failure to recoup our expenses will bring widespread disruptions to our industry sector.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:640`

## deficit.170.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.170.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

预算警告：当前合金支出已经超过了接下来数月的支出阈值。若不能解决短缺，将会给我们的工业区域造成广泛的扰乱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

Budget warning: alloy expenditures surpassing expenditures for multiple subsequent months. Failure to resolve the shortage will bring widespread disruptions to our industry sector.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:641`

## deficit.170.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.170.A&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

Scrap robots on §Y[robots_planet.GetName]§! for parts.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:642`

## deficit.170.A.tooltip

### English

Dismantle all robots on [robots_planet.GetName] (£pop£ §H[robots_planet.recycled_robots] Drones§!) to gain £alloys£ §Y[robots_planet.total_alloys_gained] Alloys§!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:643`

### English

Consolidate. Recycle. Dismantle droids on §Y[robots_planet.GetName]§!.

## deficit.170.B.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.170.B.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

拆解[robots_planet.GetName]上的一半人口（£pop£§H[robots_planet.recycled_robots]子个体§!）以获取£alloys£§Y[robots_planet.total_alloys_gained]合金§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

Dismantle half of the population on [robots_planet.GetName] (£pop£ §H[robots_planet.recycled_robots] Drones§!) to gain £alloys£ §Y[robots_planet.total_alloys_gained] Alloys§!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:645`

## deficit.175.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.175.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于无法解决合金短缺，我们被迫宣布破产并违约债务。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]正在起草新的紧缩经济计划以削减开支，避免这种情况再度发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

Having failed to resolve our alloys shortage, we have been forced to declare bankruptcy and default on our debts. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] is drafting a new austerity plan to cut expenses and preventing this situation from ever happening again.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:647`

## deficit.185.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.185.name&lang=en)

### 中文

严峻稀有水晶赤字

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

Acute Rare Crystal Deficits

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:649`

## deficit.185.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.185.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

稀有水晶在[Root.Owner.GetName]变得更加稀有了。包括关键的军用器材和先进生产设施中的核心组件，几乎各行各业都发生了短缺。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]提醒，必须在数月之内重整补给链，否则将需要大幅削减开支。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Rare crystals are becoming increasingly scarce in the [Root.Owner.GetName]. Shortages have been reported in many fields, ranging from vital military supplies to critical components used in many advanced manufacturing processes. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] warns reforms to the supply chain will be necessary within a matter of months, failing which sharp cutbacks will be necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:650`

## deficit.185.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.185.A&lang=en)

从[looted_planet.GetName]回收水晶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

Reclaim crystals from [looted_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:651`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.185.A.nomad&lang=en)

从首都方舟抽取稀有水晶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

Siphon rare crystals from the Capital Arkship.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:652`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.185.B&lang=en)

或许我们需要竭尽开采资源。

### English

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:653`

## deficit.185.C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.185.C&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以忍受“超稀有”水晶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

We can live with our crystals being rarer.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:654`

## deficit.190.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.190.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们稀有水晶的供给已经枯竭到即使关键系统都无法获得补充。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]调侃道如今它已经可以称呼为“极稀有”水晶了，他同时还强制执行了急剧的削减以期能恢复收支平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

Our supply of rare crystals has diminished to the point that even critical systems can no longer be supported. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap], which recommends that they be renamed to "rarest crystals", is enforcing harsh cutbacks to return our usage of them to sustainable levels.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:655`

## deficit.190.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.190.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的$rare_crystals$储备已消耗到无法维持多项关键功能的程度。在极端情况下，我们那些依赖这种珍贵资源的特化共生体将面临生命危险。\n\n为避免进一步的生命损失和生产力下滑，我们现在必须采取极端手段来确保系统重新恢复平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

Our supply of $rare_crystals$ has diminished to the point we can no longer maintain several critical functions. In extreme instances, we risk the lives of our more specialized symbionts who depend on this precious resource.\n\nTo prevent further loss of life and productivity, we must now rely on drastic means to ensure we once again reach equilibrium.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:656`

## deficit.200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

严峻易爆微粒赤字

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

Acute Volatile Mote Deficits

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:658`

## deficit.200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于管理缺失和大肆的浪费，我们的易爆微粒库存已经完全枯竭！这种战略资源在包括合金冶炼和军事发展等诸多领域有着广泛的应用。尽快补充库存对确保我们帝国经济繁荣至关重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

Due to mismanagement and reckless expenditures, our Volatile Motes reserves have completely evaporated! This strategic resource is widely used in multiple fields, ranging from alloys production to military development. Replenishing our supplies is of paramount importance to ensure the economic prosperity of our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:659`

## deficit.200.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.200.A&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:660`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:661`

## deficit.200.C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.200.C&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以忍受“超罕见”微粒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

We can live with our motes becoming more elusive.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:663`

## deficit.205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

长期的易爆微粒短缺导致了一场全国性的经济危机。由于无法弥补赤字，我们被迫宣布破产并违约债务。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]正在起草新的紧缩经济计划以削减开支，避免这种情况再度发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

The long-running shortage of volatile motes resulted in an empire-wide economic crash. Unable to repay our deficits, we were forced to declare bankruptcy and forfeit on our debts. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] is drafting a new financial plan, enforcing harsh cutback to rebalance our budget.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:664`

## deficit.205.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.205.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

长期的易爆微粒短缺导致了一场全国性的补给链崩溃。很显然我们已经无法继续维持我们对该物质的消耗。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]正在起草新的紧缩经济计划以削减开支，避免这种情况再度发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

The long-running shortage of volatile motes resulted in an empire-wide supply chain crash. It has become clear that we can no longer sustain our use of this material. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] is drafting a new financial plan, enforcing harsh cutback to rebalance our budget.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:665`

## deficit.205.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.205.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

我们各世界之间$volatile_motes$的短缺已造成广泛的混乱，尤其是那些生命完全依赖于它的极端微生物共生体。为了防止生命和生产力的进一步损失，我们现在必须实行剧烈的变革，以确保基本生命的延续，希望我们能尽快恢复平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

The shortage of $volatile_motes$ among our worlds has caused widespread disruption, not least to those extremophile worker-symbionts whose very lives depend on it. To prevent further loss of life and productivity, we must now institute drastic change to ensure basic continuity of life, in the hopes we might soon regain equilibrium.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:666`

## deficit.215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

严峻异星天然气赤字

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

Acute Exotic Gas Deficits

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:669`

## deficit.215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

异星天然气在我们的国库中已经成了稀罕货——而它们的长期缺失对我们所有依赖它的生产流程都产生了严重的影响。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]强烈建议我们重新考虑预算优先级，以结束这一怪异的局势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

Exotic Gases have become a foreign presence in our empire stockpile - and their continued absence is having an impact on our entire manufacturing process, which heavily depends on them. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] strongly suggests we reconsider our budget priorities to put an end to this outlandish situation.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:670`

### English

## deficit.220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的帝国已经再也无法应付异星天然气短缺对我们生产流程的毁灭性打击。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]被迫宣布破产，使我们得以违约债务。新的紧缩经济计划正在起草以削减开支，这样一来我们就可以逐渐重建我们的资源储备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

Our empire can no longer cope with the exotic gas shortages that have been disrupting our manufacturing processes. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] has been forced to declare bankruptcy, allowing us to forfeit our debts. Sharp cutbacks will be enforced, so that we may slowly rebuild our resource stockpile.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:675`

## deficit.220.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeficit.220.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的帝国已经再也无法弥补异星天然气短缺对我们生产流程的毁灭性打击。[Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap]被迫宣布破产，使我们得重新组织我们的分配系统，让我们有机会逐渐重新建立资源储备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

Our empire can no longer cope with the exotic gas shortages that have been disrupting our manufacturing processes. [Owner.GetFinancialAdvisorAgencyCap] has been forced to declare bankruptcy, allowing us to reorganize our distribution systems so that we can slowly rebuild our resource stockpile.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:676`

## leviathan_celebration_opportunity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathan_celebration_opportunity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

## situation.1000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:726`

## situation.1000.desc.small

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1000.desc.small&lang=en)

### 中文

这一天终于来临。我们的[Owner.GetSensoryWorkers]已经就位，准备为我们对§Y[This.GetTrophyName]§!进行全面的探索。随着研究的开始，我们将一小部分意识集中在§Y[Target.GetName]§!上，以消化吸收对异常项目的研究。然而，让我们将残余物拖到[Target.GetPlanetHabitat]以后发现，结果并不如我们希望的那么有趣。\n\n就在这项进程开始之后不久，我们决定将研究转移到位于[Target.GetName]的中央团簇，由特化的[Owner.GetSensoryWorkers]对其进行持续观测。希望能从中有更多的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

The day has arrived. Our [Owner.GetSensoryWorkers] were in place and ready to give us a thorough exploration of the §Y[This.GetTrophyName]§!. As the study, began a small portion of our consciousness focused on §Y[Target.GetName]§! to absorb the study of the anomalous item. However, the revelations were not as interesting as we had hoped as we dragged the remains to the [Target.GetPlanetHabitat].\n\nShortly after the process began, we decided to move the study to the center of our cluster on [Target.GetName], where the specialized [Owner.GetSensoryWorkers] will continuously observe it. Hopefully something more will come of it.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:727`

## situation.1000.desc.standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1000.desc.standard&lang=en)

### 中文

这一天终于来临。我们的[Owner.GetSensoryWorkers]已经就位，准备为我们对§Y[This.GetTrophyName]§!进行全面的探索。随着研究的开始，我们将一小部分意识集中在§Y[Target.GetName]§!上，以消化吸收对异常项目的研究。当天，整个[Owner.GetName]的产出率整整降低了§H0.2%§!。\n\n探测数据被深深映在我们的意识当中，并转移到到位于[Target.GetName]的中央团簇，由特化的[Owner.GetSensoryWorkers]对其进行持续观测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

The day has arrived. Our [Owner.GetSensoryWorkers] were in place and ready to give us a thorough exploration of the §Y[This.GetTrophyName]§!. As the study began, a significant portion of our consciousness focused on §Y[Target.GetName]§! to absorb the import of this anomalous item. Productivity in the [Owner.GetName] was reduced by a whole §H0.2%§! for the day.\n\nThe sensory data was then archived deep in our consciousness before being placed in the center of our cluster on [Target.GetName], where the specialized [Owner.GetSensoryWorkers] will continuously observe it.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:728`

## situation.1000.desc.large

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1000.desc.large&lang=en)

### 中文

这一天终于来临。大量[Owner.GetSensoryWorkers]已经就位，准备为我们对§Y[This.GetTrophyName]§!进行切实的探索。随着研究的开始，我们将整个意识集中在§Y[Target.GetName]§!上，以消化吸收对这一令我们全神贯注的异常项目的研究。几个危险境地的[owner.GetIndividualNamePlural]因一时分神而受伤，甚至还有几个失踪了。我们对此无比痴迷。\n\n在初步研究完成后，我们决定将其转移到到位于[Target.GetName]的中央团簇，由特化的[Owner.GetSensoryWorkers]对其进行持续观测。我们近乎虔诚地部署了这一研究任务，并持续分配一部分意识对其进行观测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

The day has arrived. A massive amount of [Owner.GetSensoryWorkers] were in place and ready to give us an exacting exploration of the §Y[This.GetTrophyName]§!. As the study began, for a moment, our entire consciousness focused on §Y[Target.GetName]§! to absorb the study of the anomalous item which preoccupied us so. Several [owner.GetIndividualNamePlural] caught up in precarious situations were injured in the momentary distraction, and a few were even lost. So great was our fascination.\n\nAfter the initial study was complete, we decided to move it to the center of our cluster on [Target.GetName], where the specialized [Owner.GetSensoryWorkers] will continuously observe it. We placed it down, almost reverently, and continue to dedicate a part of our consciousness to the observation.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:729`

### 中文

[This.GetLeviathanParadeName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

[This.GetLeviathanParadeName]

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:733`

## situation.1005.desc.small

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1005.desc.small&lang=en)

### 中文

今天，§Y[Target.GetName]§!首都的人群们迎来了阴沉的一天。游行的队伍携带着我们摧毁§Y[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]§!的证明，漫步经过叽叽喳喳的围观者。这一过程在全帝国范围内广播，但反响极低，[This.GetTrophyName]似乎没有我们预想的那么令人感兴趣。\n\n在完成了环球展览之后，[This.GetTrophyName]被放在了首都中心进行特展，以激励我们以及子孙后代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

An overcast day greeted the middling crowd on §Y[Target.GetName]'s§! capital city today. The procession carrying our proof of the destruction of the §Y[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]§! ambled past the meandering onlookers. Its progress was broadcast throughout our empire, but engagement was low as the [This.GetTrophyName] did not seem to be quite as enticing as we had hoped.\n\nAfter finishing the world tour, the [This.GetTrophyName] was placed in a specialized display at the center of the capital to inspire generations to come.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:734`

## situation.1005.desc.standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1005.desc.standard&lang=en)

### 中文

今天，§Y[Target.GetName]§!首都的拥挤人群们迎来了阳光明媚的一天。游行的队伍携带着我们摧毁§Y[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]§!的证明，四处环游逐一展览。游行的过程在全帝国范围内数十亿观众直播。[This.GetTrophyName]一时成为了[Owner.Species.GetAdj]文化的重要象征，激发着全[Owner.GetName]的艺术家和发明家们。\n\n在完成了环球展览之后，[This.GetTrophyName]被放在了首都中心进行特展，以激励我们以及子孙后代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

A bright day greeted the packed crowds on §Y[Target.GetName]§! today as the procession carrying our proof of the destruction of the §Y[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]§! traveled around. The parade's progress was broadcast throughout our empire to billions of viewers. The [This.GetTrophyName] already seems to have become a staple of [Owner.Species.GetAdj] culture, inspiring artists and creators across [Owner.GetName].\n\nAfter finishing the world tour, the [This.GetTrophyName] was placed in a specialized display at the center of the capital to inspire generations to come.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:735`

## situation.1005.desc.large

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1005.desc.large&lang=en)

### 中文

今天，§Y[Target.GetName]§!晴空万里令人心旷神怡，不过这是理所当然的，因为我们把大气调控系统已经满负荷开到爆了。在这伟大的一天，[Target.GetPlanetHabitat]上的每座城市的大街小巷都挤得满满当当，而更多的商旅飞船仍在不断地停泊进行星的轨道。盛大的游行逐一经过每一个定居点，迎接他们的是人山人海的街道和[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]们热情的欢呼。我们帝国中每一座能够进行大型全息投影的城市也都挤满了观看盛大全息转播的观众。作为典礼的重中之重，[This.GetTrophyName]抵达了[Target.GetName]的首都，人们已经讨论起了将今天定为每年的§Y[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]§!节日。在完成了环球展览之后，[This.GetTrophyName]被放在了首都中心进行特展，以激励我们以及子孙后代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

The weather on §Y[Target.GetName]§! was unnaturally great today, as it should have been, considering we ran our atmosphere control system on overdrive. Every city on the [Target.GetPlanetHabitat] reached its capacity, and there were still tourist ships orbiting the world just to be a part of this great day. The grand parade traveled through each settlement in turn, and was greeted by streets packed with [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Every city capable of requisitioning a holo-display in our empire was also overflowing with spectators for the grand hologram rebroadcasts. As the centerpiece of the parade, the [This.GetTrophyName], reached [Target.GetName]'s capital, people were already talking about making today a yearly §Y[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]§! inspired holiday. The [This.GetTrophyName] was then placed in a specialized display at the center of the capital to inspire generations to come.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:736`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1005.a&lang=en)

又没人来看嚷嚷着办什么庆典啊？

### English

If they won't to show up for it, why did they ask for a celebration?

## situation.1045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不稳定獠牙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

Destabilized Fang

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:741`

## situation.1045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了准备那个重要的日子，我们将一颗§Y以太巨龙的獠牙§!转运到§Y[Target.GetName]§!。把它们塞进货运飞船是一项极富挑战的工作，以太巨龙独特的生理构造似乎违背了物理法则。在进入[Target.GetPlanetHabitat]大气层时，獠牙对飞船发生了极其奇怪的反应：它在众目睽睽之下开始瓦解，要不是飞船快速应对，我们可能就会失去这一宝贵的物品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

In preparation for the big day, we have transferred one of the §YEther Drake's Fangs§! to §Y[Target.GetName]§!. Getting them into the transport ship was a challenge, as the Ether Drake's unique biology does not seem to follow the laws of physics. The fang reacted particularly strangely to the transport ship entering the [Target.GetPlanetHabitat]'s atmosphere: it began unraveling before us, and if the transport ship hadn't responded quickly, we might have lost the coveted artifact.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:742`

## situation.1045.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1045.a&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

Construct a better container and bring the fang down.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:743`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

This reaction is much more interesting than any [Owner.GetParadeStudy].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:744`

## situation.1050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

监督者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

Overseer

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:748`

## situation.1050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

摘取§Y裂爪龙之眼§!是一项混乱不堪的工程，开始阶段错误百出。幸运的是，这只巨兽有许多只眼睛让我们逐一试错。我们将这只眼睛拖到了[Target.Solar_System.GetName]以后发生一件又奇妙又恶心的事情：这只眼睛迅速地黏住了当地的传感阵列，并将自己牢牢地嵌在上面，俯视着§Y[Target.GetName]§!。事件的起因还在调查当中，但需要重申，这只巨龙的许多生理学知识仍是我们未知的。\n\n眼睛还没有和恒星基地完全融为一体，但我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]认为如果我们想继续进行之前的计划，就必须阻止这个状况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

The extraction of the §YShard's Eye§! was a messy process with a few false starts. Luckily, the behemoth had a lot of eyes for us to practice on. We then towed the optic to [Target.Solar_System.GetName], and something both fascinating and revolting happened: the oculus started attaching and integrating itself into the local sensor array overlooking §Y[Target.GetName]§!. The cause of this is unknown, but then again, much of the Drake's physiology is beyond us.\n\nThe fusing is not complete yet, but our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] believe it has to be halted now if we wish to proceed with our previous plans.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:749`

## situation.1050.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1050.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们用裂爪龙之眼审视世界吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

Let us see with the sight of The Shard!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:750`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

Cancel the [Owner.GetParadeStudy] in favor of using the eye for a different purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:751`

### English

Scrape it off, we have deadlines to reach.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1050.c&lang=en)

小心地把它取下来，我们可不想有什么磕碰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:651`

### English

Very carefully remove it, we wouldn't want to damage it.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:753`

## situation.1055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

功率骤增

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:652`

### English

Power Surge

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:754`

## situation.1055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y无畏战舰反应堆§!在运抵§Y[Target.GetName]§!之前一直是一个超级乖乖的货物，抵达后我们将其安置在一处仓库内以待那一天的到来。这下问题就来了：在无人照看的情况下，这台反应堆突然自己重启了。反应堆迸射出巨大的等离子电弧，整座仓库由内而外发出耀眼的光，整座建筑似乎很快就要被熔化了。\n\n我们[Owner.GetScientistPlural]反应迅速，力挽狂澜，于是现在我们就有了一台运转中的反应堆了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

The §YDreadnought's Reactor§! was a very well-behaved cargo until it arrived at §Y[Target.GetName]§!, where we placed it in a warehouse awaiting its big day. It was there the troubles began: while no one was looking, the reactor restarted itself. The warehouse was lit up from within as it spouted out huge arcs of plasma, threatening to melt down the entire building.\n\nThe quick actions of our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] were just sufficient to save it in the nick of time, with the upshot that we now have an active reactor to do with as we please.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:755`

### English

## situation.1060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

再生鞭毛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

Regenerating Flagella

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:760`

## situation.1060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

即便我们预警了外星污染的危险，但还是有东西搞砸了。§Y族母的鞭毛§!运入§Y[Target.GetName]§!首都时，它以某种方式接触了其中一个管运者，然后几乎是瞬间，它就发生了变化。这件战利品上长出了脉动的附肢，变成了一团搅动的肉质。慢慢地它逐渐细化，然后竟然长成了那个被接触的管运者的模样！似乎是族母的再生能力接纳了我们物种的基因，并开始产出更多的[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Even with our xeno-contamination precautions, something went wrong. As the §YMatriarch's Flagella§! entered §Y[Target.GetName]'s§! capital, it somehow got in contact with one of the handlers, and instantly, the flagella started reacting. Pulsating ripping appendages began growing from the Trophy, first forming an indistinct churning mass of flesh. Over time it became more detailed, and to our surprise, it assumed the shape of the unfortunate handler! It seems like the regenerative powers of the Matriarch have accepted the DNA of our kind and are now in the process of generating more [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:761`

## situation.1060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1060.a&lang=en)

让围观者也一起尝尝这个！

### English

Use this to feed the onlookers!

让反应自由发展吧。

### English

Let the reaction run its course.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:763`

### English

Cauterize the Flagella.

## situation.1065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

趋之若鹜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

Perpetual Pursuit of the Plume

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:765`

## situation.1065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将§Y天空龙羽翼§!带回家的时候，我们的货运飞船发现他们经过的超空间航道发生了二次扭曲。期限认为只是普通的异常现象而忽视了，直到飞船进入§Y[Target.Solar_System.GetName]§!时才发现，有一团追逐着飞船的实体导致了超空间航道膨胀：一窝龙的幼崽热切追逐着我们的货运飞船，从中鱼贯而出进入我们的星系。他们应该是躲藏在跳跃点附近跟随者我们出来航道的。这些小龙似乎没有它们亲代的智力，而是漫无目的地跟随着我们——确切地说是跟随着羽翼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

While carrying home the §YSky Dragon's Plume§!, our transportation ship noticed that the hyperlane they traveled through had a second distortion beyond their ship. Initially disregarding this as just an anomaly, it was not apparent until the ship entered §Y[Target.Solar_System.GetName]§! that a group of pursuing entities was causing the hyperlane-dilation: a clutch of dragon whelps exited into our system in hot pursuit of our transport ship. They must have lain in hiding near the jump point and followed our exit. The dragonlings do not seem to possess the mental faculties of their progenitor and seem to be aimlessly following us or, more particularly, the plume.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:766`

## situation.1070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最致密金属

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:670`

### English

The Densest of Metals

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:772`

## situation.1070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天早晨，在§Y[Target.GetName]§!上有个惊喜等着我们，§Y拾荒机器人压缩机§!在我们的储存设施里吐出了最后一块它的作品。这一大块合金比我们所能想象所能产出的最致密的合金还要紧实，仅需数立方米就可以建造出一整艘战列舰。这一喜出望外的发展让我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]相信他们有希望让压缩机以§G0.001%§!的功率重新运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

This morning, a surprise awaited us on §Y[Target.GetName]§! as the §YScavenger Bot's Compactor§! deposited the last of its creations in our storage facility. The large chunk of alloys was denser and more compact than anything we could ever hope to produce, containing enough metals in a few cubic meters to build an entire battleship. This fantastic development has our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] believing that they can get the compactor working again at §G0.001%§! efficiency, if we should wish it.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:773`

## situation.1070.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1070.a&lang=en)

把它拆了用来造点东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

Break it apart and use it for construction.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:774`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

Incorporate into the [Owner.GetParadeStudy].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:775`

### English

Cancel the [Owner.GetParadeStudy] in favor of reactivating the compactor and gaining a $d_alloys_10$ deposit in orbit of [Target.GetName].

## situation.1075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

泄露囊器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

The Leaking Sack

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:778`

## situation.1075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y星际幽魂散发器囊§!这发出的斑驳光芒看起来格外引人注目，以至于让人差点没发现这个幽魂用来产生护盾的囊发生了泄露。在快速确认里面的化学物质不会导致§Y[Target.GetName]§!生态系统崩溃之后，我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]专注于分析这些气体对我们大气的影响。\n\n似乎这些泄露的化学物质已经混入了我们大气的中间层，正在高效地散发其中凝聚的能量。如果我们继续散发其中的物质，它甚至可以有效地提高我们行星的防御能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

The strobing lights of the §YStellar Wraith's Disperser Sack§! were distracting to look upon, so distracting that it took a while to detect that the sack the Wraith used to generate its natural shield was leaking. After quickly confirming that exposure to the chemicals inside would not cause a total ecological collapse on §Y[Target.GetName]§!, our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] focused on analyzing the effects the gas had on our atmosphere.\n\nIt seems like the emitted chemicals have fused with our mesosphere and disperse concentrated energies surprisingly well. So well that if we release the rest of the disperser's contents, there would be a noticeable increase in planetary defensiveness.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:779`

## situation.1075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1075.a&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

Extract the rest of the gas and let the reaction finish.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:780`

### English

Cancel the [Owner.GetParadeStudy] in favor of infusing [Target.GetName]'s atmosphere with the $wraiths_energy_disperser$.

## situation.1080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恐怖预感

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

Horrific Premonition

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:784`

## situation.1080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetTeamMemberPlural]向[Target.Solar_System.GetName]汇报了运输§Y恐怖负相物质§!的货运飞船上的奇怪的现象。我们被包括通向纯粹苦痛与毁灭低语的世界窗口之类的恼人的幻想所袭扰。据信原因就是[This.GetLeviathanTargetName]的团块，我们有理由担忧如果将船上的这玩意带到§Y[Target.GetName]§!到底会发生什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

Our [Owner.GetTeamMemberPlural] have been reporting strange activity on the ship transporting the §YHorrific Inverse Mass§! to [Target.Solar_System.GetName]. Windows into worlds of pure pain and whispers of destruction are just a few of the disturbing accounts we have received. It's believed that the mass of the [This.GetLeviathanTargetName] is to blame, and we might rightfully worry what would happen if we were to bring the ship's cargo to §Y[Target.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:785`

## situation.1080.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1080.a&lang=en)

### 中文

算了，哪捡的扔哪去吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Return it to whence it came.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:786`

取消[Owner.GetParadeStudy]以保护[Target.GetName]上[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的安全和精神健康。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

Cancel the [Owner.GetParadeStudy] in favor of protecting the safety and sanity of the [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] on [Target.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:787`

No! We need to study this.

Contain it, we can't let this phenomenon spread.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1080.d&lang=en)

它可以赐予我们神圣的智慧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

It could teach us of the Divine.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:790`

## situation.1085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深渊一瞥

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

Glimpses of the Abyss

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:791`

## situation.1085.desc.study

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1085.desc.study&lang=en)

### 中文

此前的传言一语成谶了：§Y[This.GetName]§!上的§Y恐怖负相物质§!正在我们的现实上撕开裂口，打开通向另一个世界裂隙。这一现象突破了抑制措施，开始在[This.GetName]上蔓延。所有与这一维度接触的事物，哪怕是不经意地透过观测设备查看，都让进行观察的[Owner.GetTeamMember]感受到剧烈的痛苦。我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]担忧事态还可能进一步扩大，但同时保证只要再花一点时间他们就能解开另一个维度的奥秘了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

What was only rumors before has now been confirmed: the §YHorrific Inverse Mass§! on §Y[This.GetName]§! has been tearing holes in our very reality, opening rifts into another world. The phenomenon has breached the containment procedures and now seems to be expanding into [This.GetName]'s capital. Any contact with the dimension, even inadvertently through the lens of observational devices, causes intense distress on the observing [Owner.GetTeamMember]. Our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] are worried that his can spread even further but also promise that with just a bit more time they can unlock the secrets of the other dimension.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:792`

## situation.1085.desc.deify

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1085.desc.deify&lang=en)

### 中文

此前的传言一语成谶了：§Y[This.GetName]§!上的§Y恐怖负相物质§!正在我们的现实上撕开裂口，打开通向一个纯粹邪恶世界的裂隙。任何窥视裂隙的人都会立刻陷入疯狂，直到数个小时之后才停止尖叫。我们的牧师们长年以来都在思考着我们所击败的这一实体的存在而一无所获，如今却说他们即将顿悟了。“再给我们点时间吧！”他们恳求，然而我们的科学家警告，如果再不加以控制，事态可能会变得不可收拾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

What was only rumors before has now been confirmed; the §YHorrific Inverse Mass§! on §Y[This.GetName]§! has been tearing holes in our very reality into a world of pure evil. Anyone even glancing towards one of the rifts needs hours of solitude just to stop screaming. As our priests have been contemplating the very existence of the being we defeated for several years now without any great epiphany, they say that they are approaching one now. "We just need a bit more time!" They implore, while our scientist warn us that the process might be irreversible if we allow it to go on much further.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:793`

## situation.1085.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1085.a&lang=en)

### 中文

新的发现值得冒险！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

The discoveries are worth the risk!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:794`

### English

We need to see the true vision of the Divine.

## situation.1085.b.anathema

我们必须学会恶咒才能击败它。

### English

We must know the Anathema to defeat it.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1085.c&lang=en)

### 中文

不行，风险太大了。

### English

No, the danger is too great.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:797`

## situation.1090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空阈值

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

Threshold to the Void

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:798`

## situation.1090.desc.study

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1090.desc.study&lang=en)

### 中文

可能我们狂妄自大过了头。尽管在§Y[This.GetName]§!上的§Y恐怖负相物质§!仍封存良好，但整颗行星还是在被它从内而外逐渐撕碎。虽然我们在研究其他维度时获得的科学突破是革命性的，但它对行星的负担同样不可承受。尽管几乎没有[Owner.GetScientistPlural]有信心能控制住反应，但这并不是完全没有机会！在[This.GetName]的居民正在逐渐登船离开[This.GetPlanetHabitat]时，无数的空间裂口张开了。很快事态将会变得不可挽回。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:697`

### English

Perhaps our hubris was too great. §Y[This.GetName]§! is being ripped apart from the inside as the §YHorrific Inverse Mass§! is still sequestered on it. While the breakthroughs we have had in the sciences from studying the other dimension are revolutionary, its strain on the world has been immense. Few [Owner.GetScientistPlural] believe that they can still stabilize the reaction, but there is a chance! As inhabitants of [This.GetName] board transport ships to leave the [This.GetPlanetHabitat], an innumerable amount of rifts are opening up. The point of no return is fast approaching.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:799`

## situation.1090.desc.deify

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1090.desc.deify&lang=en)

### 中文

可能我们狂妄自大过了头。尽管在§Y[This.GetName]§!上的§Y恐怖负相物质§!仍封存良好，但整颗行星还是在被它从内而外逐渐撕碎。虽然我们对这个纯粹痛苦的维度有了许多了解，但它对行星的负担同样不可承受。几乎没有牧师敢保证自己能净化这团物质，但并不是完全没有机会！在[This.GetName]的居民正在逐渐登船离开[This.GetPlanetHabitat]时，无数的空间裂口张开了。很快事态将会变得不可挽回。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

Perhaps our hubris was too great. §Y[This.GetName]§! is being ripped apart from the inside as the §YHorrific Inverse Mass§! is still sequestered on it. While we have learned much about the dimension of pure pain, the strain on the world has been immense. Few priests still believe that they purify the mass, but there is a chance! As inhabitants of [This.GetName] board transport ships to leave the [This.GetPlanetHabitat], an innumerable amount of rifts are opening up. The point of no return is fast approaching.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:800`

## situation.1090.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1090.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做得太过头了，恐怕已经无力回天。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

We have come too far to see it end now.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:801`

## situation.1090.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1090.b&lang=en)

如果我们就此止步就是向恶咒认输！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

If we stop now the Anathema wins!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:802`

## situation.1090.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1090.c&lang=en)

### 中文

不惜一切代价解决它！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

Get rid of it, by any means necessary!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:803`

## situation.1095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

终焉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

An End

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:804`

## situation.1095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

晴空永远消失了，它不是被乌云荫蔽，而是被纯粹的虚空所笼罩。§Y[This.GetName]§!所残存的只有漂浮在虚无之海的陆地碎片。所有来不及逃离[This.GetPlanetHabitat]的人在其上的每一刻都在承受着无上的痛苦。我们为专家们争取到了尽可能多的时间。一个扭曲而恼人的声音回响在行星的上空：§R“对寸——回—回归于我——吧。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

The sky is gone. It's not covered by clouds. It has been replaced by a pure void. All that remains of §Y[This.GetName]§! is patches of ground in the ocean of nothingness. Everyone left on the [This.GetPlanetHabitat] is suffering pure agony for every instant that passes. We have bought as much time as we could for our specialists. A distorted and disturbed voice echoes from the world beyond §R"YesS re-reTURN to mee-."§!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:805`

## situation.1095.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1095.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就是现在，[Owner.GetScientistPlural]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

It's now or never, [Owner.GetScientistPlural]!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:806`

## situation.1095.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1095.b&lang=en)

### 中文

就是现在，祭司！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

It's now or never, priests!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:807`

## situation.1100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

密室逃脱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

Escape Fortress

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:809`

## situation.1100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在击败了神秘堡垒之后，它的一个舱段漂浮在太空中，我们决定将其带回[Target.Solar_System.GetName]，成为我们[Owner.GetParadeStudy]的一部分。一些[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]在进入§Y神秘堡垒舱室§!深处后都失踪了。自它运抵§Y[Target.GetName]§!时，我们每天都派出[Owner.GetTeamMemberPlural]进入探索，但今天早些时候它内部的某些结构发生了变化。舱门自发地折叠消失了，只留下无尽的舱壁嘲笑着我们的工作人员们的狂怒。我们仍能与被困的[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]取得联络，但我们目前没有想出能不破坏该结构为前提的营救方案。看来神秘堡垒还有最后一个未解之谜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

After defeating the Enigmatic Fortress, a section of it was floating in space, so we decided to bring it to [Target.Solar_System.GetName] to be a part of our [Owner.GetParadeStudy]. Several [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have gone missing inside the depths of the §YChamber from the Fortress§!. Ever since it arrived on §Y[Target.GetName]§!, we have had [Owner.GetTeamMemberPlural] exploring it daily, yet something inside the structure shifted earlier today. Doors folded in on themselves, leaving only sheer walls for our agents to rail against. We can still communicate with the trapped [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural], but we have no clear idea of getting them out without damaging the structure. It seems that the Enigmatic Fortress had one last secret.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:810`

## situation.1100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一个[Owner.GetSpeciesName]都不能少！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

Leave no [Owner.GetSpeciesName] behind!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:811`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1100.b&lang=en)

我们承担不起切割舱室的代价。

### English

We can't risk despoiling the Chamber.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:812`

### English

We could use this technology to contain our undesirables.

把这个舱室做成一个密室逃脱游戏让游行参与者来玩。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

Set the structure up as a puzzle room for parade-goers to explore.

发掘舱室的秘密！

### English

Discover the chamber's secrets!
