## situation.1110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灭世污水

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

Disastrous Drainage

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:817`

## situation.1110.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y虚空孳孽毒腺§!中的污染物发生了严重泄露！这些毁灭性的毒液已经污染了我们§Y[Target.GetName]§!的[Owner.GetWaterCoolantLower]资源，迫使我们从其他的星球运输[Owner.GetWaterCoolantLower]来缓解这场自作自受的大干旱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

The containment for the §YVoidspawn's Venom Gland§! has sprung a leak! The devastating venom has now poisoned our [Owner.GetWaterCoolantLower] supply on §Y[Target.GetName]§!, forcing us to ferry [Owner.GetWaterCoolantLower] from other worlds to alleviate the self-imposed drought.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:818`

## situation.1110.desc.reg

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.desc.reg&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.1110.desc.base$这种演化出来用以杀死硕大无朋的巨型太空兽群、溶穿厚重装甲板的毒液对任何不慎摄入的[Owner.GetSpeciesName]来说都剧毒无比。$situation.1110.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

$situation.1110.desc.base$ The venom, which evolved to incapacitate titanic Space Fauna and melt through hull plating, has proven to be incredibly toxic to any [Owner.GetSpeciesName] that has ingested it. $situation.1110.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:819`

## situation.1110.desc.lithoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.desc.lithoid&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.1110.desc.base$这种演化出来用以杀死硕大无朋的巨型太空兽群、溶穿厚重装甲板的毒液对任何不慎接触的[Owner.GetSpeciesName]沉积岩皮来说都具有极强的腐蚀性。$situation.1110.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

$situation.1110.desc.base$ The venom, which evolved to incapacitate titanic Space Fauna and melt through hull plating, has proven to be incredibly corrosive to any [Owner.GetSpeciesName] that got it on their sedimentary skin. $situation.1110.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:820`

## situation.1110.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.1110.desc.base$这种演化出来用以杀死硕大无朋的巨型太空兽群、溶穿厚重装甲板的毒液即时混进我们的冷却剂以后都是极具腐蚀性的。任何使用了这些受污染的冷却剂的单位都会立刻停止运转。$situation.1110.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

$situation.1110.desc.base$ The venom, which evolved to incapacitate titanic Space Fauna and melt through hull plating, has proven to interact corrosively when exposed to our coolant. Any units using the contaminated coolant quickly stopped functioning. $situation.1110.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:821`

## situation.1110.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在得研究如何解决这场危机。\n\n我们是要在[Target.GetPlanetHabitat]从分子层面上净化掉所有虚空孳孽的基因，或是慢慢地将这些毒液过滤掉，以继续进行我们的[Owner.GetParadeStudy]？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

We now must decide how to handle this crisis.\n\nDo we perform a molecular purge of all Voidspawn DNA on our [Target.GetPlanetHabitat], or do we slowly filter the venom out so that we can proceed with the [Owner.GetParadeStudy]?

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:822`

## situation.1110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

控制污染的扩散，保障[Owner.GetParadeStudy]继续进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

Contain the spread but don't cancel the [Owner.GetParadeStudy].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:823`

## situation.1110.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

在污染清理干净之后再举办[Owner.GetParadeStudy]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

Pause the [Owner.GetParadeStudy] until the contaminant is cleared up.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:824`

## situation.1110.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把污染物和那个毒腺一并清理出去！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:722`

### English

Cleanse the world of the contaminant and the venom sack!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:825`

## situation.1110.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1110.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

取消[Owner.GetParadeStudy]以尽快清理这颗星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

Cancel the [Owner.GetParadeStudy] in favor of quickly cleaning the world.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:826`

## situation.1115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Target.GetName]清理完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

[Target.GetName] Cleaned

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:827`

## situation.1115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在几个月的努力后，我们在§Y[Target.GetName]§!上的[Owner.GetWaterCoolantLower]资源终于移除了虚空孳孽污染变得清洁可用。这并不是徒劳一场，反之，我们在[Owner.GetWaterCoolantLower]中学到了许多异星生理学的基本知识，并且已经准备好继续进行[Owner.GetParadeStudy]。我们的[Owner.GetScientistPlural]认为，只要[Owner.GetParadeStudy]的研究得出结果，他们就能以其提升[Target.GetName]的生活质量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

After many months of effort, our [Owner.GetWaterCoolantLower] supply on §Y[Target.GetName]§! is finally clear of the Voidspawn Contaminant. It was not a wasted effort, as we have learned much from the xeno-biology present in our [Owner.GetWaterCoolantLower] and are now ready to proceed with the [Owner.GetParadeStudy]. Our [Owner.GetScientistPlural] now think that they can improve life on [Target.GetName] with the these discoveries once the [Owner.GetParadeStudy] concludes.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:828`

## situation.1120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最后的火花

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

The Last Spark

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:830`

## situation.1120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们遗留在噬星者最终被击杀的地方的探测器汇报，那具遗骸的一部分开始和恒星融为一体。释放的能量增进了恒星活动，但我们的计算表明，由于移除了§Y卵鞘§!，这仍不足以让§Y[dead_stellarite.solar_system.GetName]§!恒星系恢复原有的光明。如果我们让货运飞船返回，我们有希望可以重新点亮这颗恒星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

The observation probes we had left behind at the site of the Stellar Devourer's final demise report that portions of the body have started to merge back into the star. The energy seems to have increased stellar activity, but with the removal of the §YEgg Sac§!, our calculations state that it won't be enough to restore the §Y[dead_stellarite.solar_system.GetName]§! system to its former luster. If we turn our transport ship back now, we could still potentially reignite the star.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:831`

## situation.1120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

归还卵鞘，并看看我们能做些什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

Return the Egg Sac and see what can be done.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:832`

## situation.1120.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1120.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

取消[Owner.GetParadeStudy]以尝试重新点亮[dead_stellarite.system.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:729`

### English

Cancel the [Owner.GetParadeStudy] in favor of attempting to reignite the [dead_stellarite.system.GetName] star.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:833`

## situation.1120.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1120.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不，我们要把卵鞘带到[Target.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

No, we are bringing the Egg Sac to [Target.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:834`

## situation.1125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

飞梭扑火

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:731`

### English

Transverse Orientation

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:836`

## situation.1125.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1125.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n他们的货物，数以百万计的微型噬星者，在绝对零度下储存，以确保它们稳定而惰性，直到将他们送到恒星系的主恒星。货运飞船需要继续保持静默飞行一段时间，以便进一步渗透[Target.GetSpeciesAdj]首都。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:732`

### English

\n\nTheir cargo - millions of tiny Stellar Devourers - is stored at absolute zero, which keeps them docile and inert until they are ready to be introduced to the system's primary star. The transport ship will have to go dark for a while as they further infiltrate the [Target.GetSpeciesAdj] capital.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:837`

## situation.1125.desc.binary

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1125.desc.binary&lang=en)

### 中文

[Target.GetAdj]双星系统的光芒照耀着我们的货运飞船驶入[Target.capital_scope.Solar_System.GetName]。$situation.1125.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

The light of the [Target.GetAdj] twin stars shone down on our transport ship as it entered the [Target.capital_scope.Solar_System.GetName] system.$situation.1125.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:838`

## situation.1125.desc.trinary

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1125.desc.trinary&lang=en)

### 中文

构成[Target.GetName]核心的星光三重奏照耀着我们的货运飞船的船体驶入[Target.capital_scope.Solar_System.GetName]星系。$situation.1125.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

The trio of lights that form the heart of the [Target.GetName] glinted off our transport ship's hull as it entered the S[Target.capital_scope.Solar_System.GetName] system.$situation.1125.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:839`

## situation.1125.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1125.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

在踏出了驶向赐予[Target.capital_star.GetName]生命恒星的旅途的第一步之后，我们的货运飞船仍在继续挺进[Target.GetName]宇域。$situation.1125.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:735`

### English

After having finished the first leg of their journey to the life-giving [Target.capital_star.GetName] star, our transport ship ventures further into [Target.GetName] space.$situation.1125.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:840`

## situation.1130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

旧版密码

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

Older Codes

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:842`

## situation.1130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

运载噬星者的货运飞船已经通过了恒星系的安全检查，其中使用的密码是此前由$role_spymaster$[spynetwork.leader.GetName]所捕获的，这对顺利通行至关重要。我们的操作员已经开始将这些噬星者宝宝重新解冻以恢复其活动。货运飞船将再次瞄准恒星系的首要恒星，作为这次旅途的终点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

Our Stellar Devourer transport has cleared the system's security check - the security codes that $role_spymaster$ [spynetwork.leader.GetName] has been gathering were crucial for passing into the system. Our operatives have started the heating procedure to get the baby Stellar Devourers moving. They will again aim for the system's primary star for the transport ship's final voyage.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:843`

## situation.1135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

离开货运飞船，进入燃烧的等离子体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

Out of the Transport Ship, Into the Burning Plasma

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:845`

## situation.1135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经收到来自$role_spymaster$[spynetwork.leader.GetName]的密电：一切就绪。我们的操作员已经开始弃船操作，只保留下核心船员。一旦舰船加速到最大的亚光速航速，他们也会在飞船撞击这个星系的首要恒星之前脱离，以确保我们不留下任何蛛丝马迹。\n\n噬星者宝宝们已经开始躁动不安。如果我们再不快点执行操作，它们可能都要开始打起飞船燃料的主意了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

We have received word back from $role_spymaster$ [spynetwork.leader.GetName]: everything is ready. Our operatives have already started abandoning the transport ship, leaving only a skeleton crew behind. Once the ship hits its maximum sublight speed, they too will escape just before the transport crashes into the system's primary star, ensuring we leave no evidence behind.\n\nThe Stellar Devourers have started to get quite agitated. If we don't commence the operation soon, they might make their way into the transport's fuel supply.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:846`

## situation.1140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

成功感染

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

Successful Infestation

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:848`

## situation.1140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们干预行动的最后证据很快在恒星的日冕层熔化了。我们的传感器在撞击后的一瞬间焚毁，但这足以证明我们已经成功地感染了§Y[From.target.capital_star.GetName]§!。\n\n我们已经为这个恒星系写下了残酷而寒冷的终章。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

The last proof of our tampering quickly melted inside the star's corona. Our sensors burned up moments after impact, but that was enough to confirm that we had successfully infested §Y[From.target.capital_star.GetName]§!.\n\nWe may just have doomed an entire solar system to a cruel, cold death.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:849`

## situation.1140.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1140.a&lang=en)

### 中文

拭目以待吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

Now we wait.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:850`

## situation.1145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恒星能量亏损

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

Solar Energy Wanes

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:852`

## situation.1145.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1145.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[capital_star.GetName]遭遇了一场异常的恒星活动低谷期。由于温度的降低，恒星黑子以史无前例的数量大量出现，让恒星的辐射发生了非常明显的衰弱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

We are experiencing an unusually inactive solar cycle from [capital_star.GetName]. Sunspots have appeared in unprecedented numbers because of the reduced temperature of our star, leading to a noticeable decrease in solar radiation.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:853`

## situation.1145.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1145.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.1145.desc.base$\n\n[capital_scope.GetName]上的居民已经开始囤积核热毯，减少外出。希望我们能尽早回到温暖的日子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

$situation.1145.desc.base$\n\nInhabitants of [capital_scope.GetName] have taken to stocking up on fission-powered blankets and minimizing trips outside. Hopefully, we will return to warmer times soon.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:854`

## situation.1145.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1145.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.1145.desc.base$\n\n我们已经分派了更多的能量用于产热，并指示所有子个体凑成团簇一同行动以减少热量散失。我们一定能熬过去的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

$situation.1145.desc.base$\n\nWe have allocated more energy to our heating budget and have instructed all drones to huddle closer to each other to preserve heat. We will endure this.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:855`

## situation.1145.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1145.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.1145.desc.base$\n\n尽管这场骚动触动了我们共同本能深处的恐惧，但鉴于我们知晓母星此前造成的破坏：我们将坚持下去。我们已分配更多能量用于供暖我们的庇护所，而我们的共生体们也将学会为了取暖而更加紧密地依偎在一起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:747`

### English

$situation.1145.desc.base$\n\nThough this disturbance strikes fear into the core of our shared instincts, knowing the damage our home star has wreaked before: we shall endure. We have allocated more energy for heating our sheltered dwellings, and our symbionts shall learn to huddle closer to each other for warmth.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:856`

## situation.1145.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1145.a&lang=en)

### 中文

把暖气开到最大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

Crank up our heaters.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:857`

## situation.1150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

凛冬已至

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

Great Chill

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:859`

## situation.1150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[capital_star.GetName]§!内部的聚变反应持续低迷的时长前所未见。由于其辐照能量的骤减，[capital_scope.GetName]上已经发生了多种生态灾害，如果继续下去，我们可能将面临冰河时期。所有的空间站和定居点都遭遇到了相似的情况。我们的[owner.GetScientistPlural]确信一定有什么蹊跷，希望能研究[capital_star.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:750`

### English

The fusion chain reaction inside of §Y[capital_star.GetName]§! has now been on a steady downturn for an unprecedented amount of time. There are active ecological disasters on [capital_scope.GetName] due to the lowered radiation, and if this continues, we might be looking at an ice age. All stations and other places of residence in the system are suffering from similar issues. Our [owner.GetScientistPlural] are sure that there is something abnormal afoot and desire to investigate [capital_star.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:860`

## situation.1150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

查查到底发生了什么事！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

Figure out what is going on!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:861`

## situation.1160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现寄生虫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

Parasites Found

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:863`

## situation.1160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§R[capital_star.GetName]被微型噬星者感染了！§!我们不明白这些星神兽幼体到底是怎么跑到我们的恒星上的，明明它们不具有任何超光速能力。我们可以在未来对其进行研究，但当下我们最要紧的是研究如何停止它们的寄生，否则我们的整个首都就要被彻底冻死了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

§R[capital_star.GetName] is infested with tiny Stellar Devourers!§! We have no clue how these neonate leviathans have managed to get to our star as they don't seem to possess any FTL capabilities. We can uncover all that later. For now, we need to discover a way to stop the parasites before our capital freezes over.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:864`

## situation.1160.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1160.a&lang=en)

### 中文

好，把它们赶出去！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

Well, get them out of there!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:865`

## situation.1170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Target.GetName]熄灭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

[Target.GetName] Extinguished

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:867`

## situation.1170.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1170.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

今天对[owner.GetName]而言是地狱般的一天，我们的首都恒星§Y[target.GetName]§!熄灭了。尽管有数百万人撤离，但这对于我们遭受的打击来说不过杯水车薪。我们的首都永远地陷入了冰河时期。[target.GetName]则缩水成了一颗褐矮星，这就意味着这个恒星系里任何生命都不再适合生存了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

Today was a terrible day for the [owner.GetName], as our capital star §Y[target.GetName]§! went out. Despite having evacuated many millions it only slightly softens the blow. Our capital is forever lost to a new and permanent ice age. [target.GetName] itself has shrunken to become a brown dwarf star, meaning life remaining in the system will permanently be diminished.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:868`

## situation.1170.desc.notknows

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1170.desc.notknows&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.1170.desc.base$\n很遗憾，究竟是什么把这些噬星者带到我们星系的仍不得而知。目前是灾后重建的时候了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:757`

### English

$situation.1170.desc.base$\nSadly, this freak occurrence that delivered the Stellar Devourers to our system will remain a mystery. For now, we must rebuild.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:869`

## situation.1170.desc.knows

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1170.desc.knows&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.1170.desc.base$\n我们推测这些噬星者很有可能是由§Y[star_infesters.GetName]§!送到我们的恒星上的，现在我们要他们血债血偿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

$situation.1170.desc.base$\nWe know that these Devourers were purposely introduced to our star by the §Y[star_infesters.GetName]§! - and now they shall suffer.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:870`

## situation.1170.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1170.a&lang=en)

### 中文

可怕的惨剧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

A terrible tragedy.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:871`

## situation.1170.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.1170.b&lang=en)

### 中文

[star_infesters.GetName]必将血债血偿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

The [star_infesters.GetName] shall pay.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:872`

## ether_trophy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aether_trophy&lang=en)

### 中文

$drakes_scale$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

$drakes_scale$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:876`

## ether_trophy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aether_trophy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y以太巨龙§!的尖牙是这个星球首府的重中之重。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

The fang of an §YEther Drake§! is the centerpiece of this world's capital.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:877`

## studying_decaying_fang

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astudying_decaying_fang&lang=en)

### 中文

研究腐坏的尖牙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

Studying the Decaying Fang

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:878`

## studying_decaying_fang_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astudying_decaying_fang_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y以太巨龙§!的尖牙正在我们大气层的附近逐渐腐坏，对其进行研究能够让我们了解这个宇宙之前有什么存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

Studying the §YEther Drake's§! fang as it decays near our atmospheres gives us insights into whatever existed before this universe did.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:879`

## all_seeing_shard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aall_seeing_shard&lang=en)

### 中文

全知裂爪龙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

All Seeing Shard

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:881`

## all_seeing_shard_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aall_seeing_shard_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过亵渎的机械—异星生物学融合技术，这个恒星系的传感阵列可以触及的范围远远超过正常手段能够到达的极限。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:767`

### English

By the unholy fusion of machine and xeno-biology, the sensor arrays in this system can reach far beyond what we normally would be able to.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:882`

## shard_trophy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashard_trophy&lang=en)

### 中文

$eye_of_the_shard$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

$eye_of_the_shard$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:883`

## shard_trophy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashard_trophy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y裂爪龙§!不停监视的眼睛之下，这个星球的人群工作起来更加卖力了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

Under the watchful eye of §YThe Shard§! the population on this world seem to work harder.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:884`

## dread_trophy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adread_trophy&lang=en)

### 中文

$dreadnoughts_reactor$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

$dreadnoughts_reactor$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:886`

## dread_trophy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adread_trophy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y自动无畏战舰§!上拆卸下来的反应堆正给这个星球的一部分供能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

A reactor from the §YAutomated Dreadnought§! partially powers this world.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:887`

## reactor_overdrive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areactor_overdrive&lang=en)

### 中文

反应堆过载

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:772`

### English

Reactor Overdrive

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:888`

## reactor_overdrive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areactor_overdrive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

让§Y自动无畏战舰§!反应堆暂时性涨高能量，可以为这个星球的每一个岗位提供能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

## operation_consume_star_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_consume_star_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我国拥有$devourers_plasma$，就可以驯养一批微型噬星者。我们的任务是决定把它们丢到哪颗恒星上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

With the $devourers_plasma$ within our empire, we have access to a horde of tiny Stellar Devourers. We just need to decide which star to consume.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1008`

## operation_consume_star_on_success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_consume_star_on_success&lang=en)

### 中文

$IF_SUCCESSFUL|E$\n用噬星者感染[target.GetName]的母恒星，并将其逐渐吞噬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

$IF_SUCCESSFUL|E$\nInfest [target.GetName] capital star with Stellar Devourers, which will slowly consume the star.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1009`

## operation_consume_star_black_hole

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_consume_star_black_hole&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

The target empire's capital orbits a black hole, leaving our Stellar Devourers nothing to eat.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1010`

## operation_consume_star_no_dysonsphere

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_consume_star_no_dysonsphere&lang=en)

## war_goal_wg_revenge_for_eaten_star_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awar_goal_wg_revenge_for_eaten_star_desc&lang=en)

## situation_kaleidoscope_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_kaleidoscope_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一种五彩斑斓的能源寄生虫正在侵染我们的能源电网，提高了我们的能源供给。我们可以放任它，或者通过§Y谍报行动§!将这个麻烦传导到其他帝国。\n\n[GetKaleidoscopeMood]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:888`

### English

A colorful energy parasite is infecting our power grid, feasting on our energy supplies. We could indulge it, or use an §YEspionage Operation§! to pass this problem to another empire.\n\n[GetKaleidoscopeMood].

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1031`

## situation_kaleidoscope_monthly_change_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_kaleidoscope_monthly_change_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

让§H万华镜§!寄生虫挨饿会降低它的§Y心情§!，提高负面事件的发生几率。给它投喂会提高它的心情，维持它愉悦和稳定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:889`

### English

Starving the §HKaleidoscope§! will lower its §Ymood§!, increasing the chance of negative events. Feeding it will increase it, keeping it appeased and placid.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1032`

## situation_unknown_outcome_kaleidoscope

### English

The Kaleidoscope's §Ymood§! will determine the final outcome.

## r_vacuum_flower_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_vacuum_flower_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜的花朵如梦似幻、稍纵即逝，其花瓣处于不断变化的状态，具有操纵、吸收和反射光波的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:914`

### English

The Kaleidoscope's bloom is a fragile florescence of ever-shifting petals, possessing the ability to manipulate, absorb, and reflect light waves.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1064`

## relic_vacuum_flower_active

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelic_vacuum_flower_active&lang=en)

### 中文

依据你的首都恒星系的§Y恒星类型§!，获取持续10年的不同类型产出加成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

### English

Grants a different resource production bonus for 10 years depending on the §YStar Class§! of your capital system.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1065`

### English

## concept_vacuum_flower_star_effects_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_vacuum_flower_star_effects_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据首都星系恒星类型产生加成：\n\n—§YB型§!：§G+75§!£alloys£合金\n—§YA型§!：§G+150§!£consumer_goods£消费品\n—§YF型§!：§G+300§!£food£食物\n—§YG型§!：§G+300§!£energy£能量\n—§YK型§!：§G+300§!£minerals£矿物\n—§YM型§!：§G+30§!£rare_crystals£稀有水晶\n—§Y红巨星§!：§G+15§!£sr_living_metal£活体金属\n—§Y褐矮星§!：§G+30§!£volatile_motes£易爆微粒\n—§Y脉冲星§!：§G+15§!£sr_zro£\n—§Y黑洞§!：§G+15§!£sr_dark_matter£暗物质\n—§Y中子星§!：§G+15§!£nanites£纳米机器人\n—§Y翠光使§!：§G+30§!£exotic_gases£异星天然气\n—§Y古代裂隙§!：§G+15§!£astral_threads£星界丝缕\n\n双星和三星系统提供组成恒星类型效果的总和。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

The bonus granted depends on the star class of your capital system:\n\n- §YClass B§!: §G+75§! £alloys£ Alloys\n- §YClass A§!: §G+150§! £consumer_goods£ Consumer Goods\n- §YClass F§!: §G+300§! £food£ Food\n- §YClass G§!: §G+300§! £energy£ Energy\n- §YClass K§!: §G+300§! £minerals£ Minerals\n- §YClass M§!: §G+30§! £rare_crystals£ Rare Crystals\n- §YRed Giant§!: §G+15§! £sr_living_metal£ Living Metal\n- §YBrown Dwarf§!: §G+30§! £volatile_motes£ Volatile Motes\n- §YPulsar§!: §G+15§! £sr_zro£ Zro\n- §YBlack Hole§!: §G+15§! £sr_dark_matter£ Dark Matter\n- §YNeutron Star§!: §G+15§! £nanites£ Nanites\n- §YViridescent Lightbringer§!: §G+30§! £exotic_gases£ Exotic Gases\n- §YAncient Rift§!: §G+15§! £astral_threads£ Astral Threads\n\nBinary and trinary systems grant the combined effects of their component star types.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1068`

Color Out of Space

## situation.2003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人民从轨道上看到的是§H[capital_scope.GetName]§!突然关停，城市内的每个光源都被熄灭。而§H[capital_scope.GetName]§!的居民所经历的更糟糕：车辆变成了路障，工厂停止运转，医院设备处于无响应的状态。这是一次全球性的突然停电，突如其来，莫名其妙。\n\n当寂静和阴影笼罩着我们的星球时，奇怪的光芒在天空中绽放，将我们的首都恒星系包围在万华镜般的迷雾中。这两个事件无疑是相关的，但我们的[root.GetTechnicianPlural]仍然在抓瞎——是的，字面意义上的抓瞎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:940`

### English

What our people saw from orbit was §H[capital_scope.GetName]§! shutting down, every light source within the city snuffed out at once. What people on §H[capital_scope.GetName]§! experienced was much worse: vehicles reduced to road blocks, factories grinding to a halt, hospital equipment lifeless and inert. A world-wide blackout, as sudden as it was inexplicable.\n\nAs silence and shadows veiled our world, strange light patterns bloomed in the sky, enveloping our capital system in a kaleidoscopic mist. The two events are doubtlessly related, but our [root.GetTechnicianPlural] are still - literally - in the dark.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1095`

## situation.2003.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2003.a&lang=en)

### 中文

恢复我们的电网功能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:941`

### English

Restore our power grid.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1096`

## situation.2004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的传感器报告万华镜寄生虫发光图样的幻影出现在了§H[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]§!恒星系。这个现象的自然机理暂时还不清楚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:945`

### English

Our sensors report the apparition of kaleidoscopic light patterns in the §H[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]§! system. The nature of the phenomenon is unclear.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1101`

## situation.2004.desc.again

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2004.desc.again&lang=en)

### 中文

我们先前观察到的万华镜幻影已经移动到了§H[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]§!恒星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:947`

### English

The kaleidoscopic apparition we previously observed has moved to the §H[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1103`

## situation.2005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大停电

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:949`

### English

Blackout

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1106`

## situation.2005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在自己的电网系统中找不到任何缺陷。我们的发电机正在产生能量，但是某种存在……某种存在正在吸收它。这可能是我们在天空中能够观察到光图案的结果，这个图案的几何性质透露出一定的智慧痕迹。\n\n这个东西——这个活着的§Y万华镜§!——是一个满怀恶意的存在，还是一团无意识的迷雾？答案尚未明确。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:950`

### English

We can find no defects in our power grid. Our generators are producing energy, but something is... absorbing it. It may be a result of the light patterns witnessed in our skies, the geometric nature of which betray an intelligent design.\n\nIs this thing - this living §YKaleidoscope§! - a malicious entity, or just a mindless mist? The answer is unclear.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1107`

## situation.2005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

试图将它从我们的电网中剥离出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:951`

### English

Attempt to detach it from our power grid.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1108`

## situation.2010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗伊始

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:953`

### English

Black Start

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1111`

## situation.2010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多亏了我们的[owner.GetTechnicianPlural]和[owner.GetScientistPlural]的通力合作，我们设法将能量寄生虫从我们的电力网络中剔除了。我们最漫长的黑夜已经过去，但是§Y万华镜§!寄生虫仍然在我们的头顶上闪耀，渴望能量。\n\n我们无法与它进行交流，常规武器对这类生物无效。我们剩下的选项是给它提供足够的能量以维持其心情满足，或者试图让它挨饿。我们的特工还建议组织一次§Y谍报行动§!，将它引诱到另一个帝国。让其他人来处理它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:954`

### English

Thanks to the coordinated effort of our [owner.GetTechnicianPlural] and [owner.GetScientistPlural], the energy parasite has been cut from our power grid. Our longest night is over, but §Ythe Kaleidoscope§! still shines upon us, hungry for energy.\n\nWe have no way to communicate with it, and conventional weaponry won't work on a being of this kind. Our remaining options are to feed it just enough to keep it appeased, or try to starve it. Our operatives also suggested organizing an §YEspionage Operation§! to lure it towards another empire. Let someone else deal with it.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1112`

## situation.2010.desc.again

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2010.desc.again&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人民从轨道上看到的是一个熟悉的图案再次出现：§H[capital_scope.GetName]§!上的所有电源突然关闭，我们的首都受到全球停电的打击，而熹微的光亮在天空中跃动。\n§Y万华镜§!回来了！我们曾经将这个能量寄生虫诱骗到其他国家，现在又给送了回来。\n\n根据我们专家的分析，投资建设一个§Y$building_planetary_shield_generator$§!能够防止我们的首都再次成为目标，但是一切都为时已晚：因为能量寄生虫已经待这儿不走了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:955`

### English

What our people saw from orbit was the repetition of a familiar pattern: all power on §H[capital_scope.GetName]§! shutting down, our capital hit by a worldwide blackout while shimmering lights danced in the sky.\n§YThe Kaleidoscope§! has returned! The energy parasite we once foisted onto others has been sent back to us.\n\nAccording to our experts, investing in a §Y$building_planetary_shield_generator$§! will prevent our capital from being targeted again, but it's too late for now: the energy parasite is here to stay.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1113`

## situation.2011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

依照我们经过验证和测试的流程，我们将万华镜寄生虫从§H[root.GetName]§!的能量电网中剥离，恢复了首都的供电。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:962`

### English

Following our proven and tested procedures, we detached the Kaleidoscope from §H[root.GetName]§!'s energy grid, restoring power to our capital.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1121`

## situation.2020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

渗入能量电网

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:965`

### English

Infiltrating the Energy Grid

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1125`

## situation.2020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的特工通过假扮成技工成功渗透进入了§H[target.GetName]§!的能源区划。他们的下一项任务是安装一个返航信标，诱惑能量寄生虫前来这个行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:966`

### English

By posing as technicians, our operatives successfully infiltrated the §H[target.GetName]§!'s energy districts. Their next assignment is to install a homing beacon to lure the energy parasite to the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1126`

## situation.2021.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2021.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天清空净

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:968`

### English

Clear Skies

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1129`

## situation.2021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们天空中的异光消散了，提前宣告了我们的特工之后汇报的事情：他们激活了能量信标，将§Y万华镜§!寄生虫引走了！\n\n现在就让§H[target.GetName]§!去处理这麻烦吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:969`

### English

The lights have faded from our skies, first announcing what our intelligence operatives later confirmed: our operators activated the energy beacon, luring §Ythe Kaleidoscope§! away!\n\nLet the §H[target.GetName]§! deal with it now.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1130`

## situation.2025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

嫁祸于人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:970`

### English

Offloading The Problem

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1132`

## situation.2025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

依据我们的谍报信息，§H[kaleidoscope_new_owner.GetName]§!，目前正遭遭受到能源短缺的困扰。我们送他们的§Y万华镜§!似乎正是这一经济困境的主要起因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:971`

### English

According to our espionage reports, §H[kaleidoscope_new_owner.GetName]§!, is currently suffering from an energy shortage. The §YKaleidoscope§! we gifted them seems to be the main cause of their economic troubles.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1133`

## situation.2030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

轻度侵染

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:974`

### English

Minor Infestation

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1137`

## situation.2030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一只饥肠辘辘的万华镜寄生虫将双齿深深扎进了§H[event_target:infested_planet.GetName]§!的能量电网中。随之而来的全殖民地范围的大停电颇为剧烈，但与此前的攻击并不相似。我们已经知道怎么对付这东西了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:975`

### English

A starved Kaleidoscope has sunk its metaphorical teeth into §H[event_target:infested_planet.GetName]§!'s energy grid. The ensuing colony-wide blackout was dramatic, but not dissimilar from previous attacks. We know how to deal with this.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1138`

## situation.2031.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2031.name&lang=en)

### 中文

电击疗法

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:977`

### English

Electroshock

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1141`

## situation.2031.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2031.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一只贪婪的万华镜正试图强行连接到我们在§H[target.GetName]§!上的§Y能量中枢§!。这提供了一个研究这只生物独特能力的绝佳机会，但我们预计这座建筑将因此受到严重破坏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:978`

### English

A ravenous Kaleidoscope is trying to forcibly connect itself to one our §YEnergy Nexus§! on §H[target.GetName]§!. This presents a remarkable opportunity to study the being's unique abilities, but we expect the building will be severely damaged as a result.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1142`

## situation.2031.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2031.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就让它吃点我们的能量吧。为了科学！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:979`

### English

Let it eat our energy. For Science!

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1143`

## situation.2031.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2031.b&lang=en)

### 中文

偷一些能量回来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:980`

### English

Steal some energy back.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1144`

## situation.2032.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2032.name&lang=en)

### 中文

焦躁模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:982`

### English

Unnerving Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1147`

## situation.2032.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2032.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜焦躁地颤动着，让我们的天空充满了不和谐、不均匀的光线。我们的人不禁觉得这种景象令人不安。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:983`

### English

The Kaleidoscope trembles nervously, filling our skies with jarring, uneven patterns of light. Our people can't help but find the spectacle unnerving.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1148`

## situation.2033.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2033.name&lang=en)

### 中文

噩兆模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:985`

### English

Ominous Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1151`

## situation.2033.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2033.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜犹如一道敞开的窗口，让我们的天空充满了不祥而病态的图案。我们的祭祀认为这是凶兆。也许明智的做法是牺牲一些能量给这个生物，消除它的饥饿，平息我们人民的恐惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

The Kaleidoscope throbs like an open wound, filling our skies with ominous, sickly patterns. Our priests believe it an ill omen. It may be wise to sacrifice some energy to the creature, to quench its hunger and appease our people's fears.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1152`

## situation.2033.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2033.a&lang=en)

### 中文

向万华镜供给能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:987`

### English

Offer Energy to the Kaleidoscope.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1153`

## situation.2033.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2033.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们绝不惯着它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:988`

### English

We shall not bend to its will.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1154`

## situation.2034.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2034.name&lang=en)

### 中文

幻吸模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:990`

### English

Pilfering Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1157`

## situation.2034.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2034.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜号渴求能量，它学会了如何从我们的舰队中吸取能量。尽管能量消耗很小，但对我们的航速有明显的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:991`

### English

Hungry for energy, the Kaleidoscope has learned how to siphon energy from our fleet. The power drain is minimal, but is having a detectable impact on our ships' speed.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1158`

## situation.2037.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2037.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宽慰模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:993`

### English

Soothing Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1161`

## situation.2037.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2037.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

开心的万华镜让[owner.capital_scope.GetName]的天空中涂满了令人愉悦的图形，一个个分型互相流变，带来了一波波变化万千的奇观。\n\n整颗星球仿佛静止了。人们驻足欣赏，为之惊叹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:994`

### English

A happy Kaleidoscope fills [owner.capital_scope.GetName]'s skies with pleasing shapes, fractals melting into other fractals in ever-changing waves of wonder.\n\nThe planet stops. Admires. Breathes. 

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1162`

## situation.2038.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2038.name&lang=en)

### 中文

眩目模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:996`

### English

Dazzling Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1165`

## situation.2038.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2038.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜不断变化的图案会不会是一种沟通的方式？或者这只是一种类似呼吸一般的自发流程？我们的[root.GetScientistPlural]尚未找到答案。万华镜也正在为我们带来不可估量的研究数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

Could the shifting patterns of the Kaleidoscope be a form of communication? Or are they an autonomous process, more akin to breathing? Our [root.GetScientistPlural] still don't have an answer. The Kaleidoscope is providing us with an invaluable amount of scientific data.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1166`

## situation.2041.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2041.name&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜之花

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1000`

### English

Blooming Kaleidoscope

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1170`

## situation.2041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有幸得出体验星系最壮观的灯光秀：万华镜的花瓣往复折叠、盛开、绽放！然而，我们的仪器则带来了一则不那么令人高兴的消息。\n\n这种能量生物变得如此充满力量，它甚至像一颗垂死的恒星一样发生了坍缩。在它的废墟中，我们的探测器发现了一个外形非常类似花朵的晶体团簇。根据初步研究，这种“花”可以放大最微弱的光线，在这个过程中打破了一些已知的物理定律。我们的[root.owner.GetScientistPlural]推测，这种花不过是一座光线可以被无限地反射叠加的多维镜屋中可见的冰山一角。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1001`

### English

We have been blessed to experience the galaxy's most splendid light show: kaleidoscopic petals folding into themselves; blooming; exploding! However, our instruments tell a less triumphant story.\n\nThe energy being became so full of power it collapsed into itself like a dying star. From the devastation, our probes retrieved a crystalline cluster, shaped very much like a flower blossom. According to preliminary studies, the 'flower' can magnify the intensity of the faintest light ray, breaking a few known laws of physics in the process. Our [root.owner.GetScientistPlural] hypothesize the flower is merely the visible part of a larger construct - a multi-dimensional house of mirrors where light beams are infinitely reflected and recombined.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1171`

## situation.2042.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2042.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.2041.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1003`

### English

$situation.2041.name$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1174`

## situation.2042.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2042.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器报告§H[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]§!恒星系中万华镜的图案发生了剧烈的绽放，并在五彩斑斓的爆炸中消失了。那场面一定相当壮观。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1004`

### English

Sensors report the kaleidoscopic pattern in the §H[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]§! system just bloomed, disappearing in a final burst of colorful patterns. Must have been quite the spectacle.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1175`

## situation.2043.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2043.name&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜爆炸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1006`

### English

Exploding Kaleidoscope

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1178`

## situation.2043.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2043.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜自我折叠的一瞬，它看起来就像一朵最精致的水晶花。然而，随之而来的爆炸一点也不温和：一股灼热的爆炸席卷了整个星系，我们所有的电子设备都发生了过载，烧焦了能源电网。\n\n维修工作已经在进行中，但我们还需要一段时间才能适应这片空旷的天空。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1007`

### English

When the Kaleidoscope folded into itself, just for a second, it looked like the most delicate crystalline flower. However, the ensuing explosion was anything but gentle: a coruscating boom that scorched the system and overloaded all our electronic devices, frying our energy grid.\n\nRepairs are already underway, but it will take some time for us to get used to the newly empty sky.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1179`

## situation.2044.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2044.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$situation.2043.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1009`

### English

$situation.2043.name$

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1182`

## situation.2044.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2044.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器报告在§H[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]§!恒星系的万华镜图案爆炸了，释放了一道能量冲击波损坏了星系中所有的设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1010`

### English

Sensors report the kaleidoscopic pattern in the §H[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]§! system just exploded, releasing an energy shockwave that doubtlessly damaged every structure in the system.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1183`

## situation.2040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天清空净

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1012`

### English

Clear Skies

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1186`

## situation.2040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在在最后一阵红色的起伏图案爆发之后，万华镜消失了，它的彩色卷须像雾一样消散了。\n\n在它的光芒下度过了这么长时间后，我们的天空看起来空落落的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1013`

### English

After a final burst of red, undulating patterns, the Kaleidoscope faded away, its colorful tendrils dissipating like mist.\n\nAfter so much time spent beneath its lights, our skies look almost empty.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1187`

## situation.2045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天清空净

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1015`

### English

Clear Skies

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1190`

## situation.2045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器回报我们在[kaleidoscope_system.GetName]恒星系探测到的万华镜图案已经消退了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1016`

### English

Sensors report the kaleidoscopic pattern we detected in the [kaleidoscope_system.GetName] system has faded away.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1191`

## situation.2048.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2048.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无尽的饥饿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1018`

### English

Endless Hunger

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1194`

## situation.2048.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2048.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜寄生虫事实证明是一个异常好相处的邻居，但是要让它持续保持满足的心态不是一件容易的事情。建一个戴森球能不能满足它对能量的那种无底洞般的胃口呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1019`

### English

The Kaleidoscope has proven to be a strangely amiable neighbor, but keeping it sated has proven to be a challenge. Would a Dyson Sphere quench its endless thirst for energy?

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1195`

## situation.2048.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2048.a&lang=en)

### 中文

为万华镜寄生虫提供一个改版戴森球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1020`

### English

Modify a Dyson Sphere for the Kaleidoscope.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1196`

## situation.2048.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2048.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

解锁一项新的戴森球升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1021`

### English

Unlock a new Dyson Sphere upgrade.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1197`

## situation.2048.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2048.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能浪费能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1022`

### English

We can't spare the energy.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1198`

## situation.2049.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2049.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奇迹球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1024`

### English

Wonder Sphere

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1201`

## situation.2049.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation.2049.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万华镜寄生虫盘附在了我们提供的改版戴森球上，吞食着一整个恒星的能量，其不断延展的、色彩缤纷的花瓣包覆了整个恒星系。根据我们的数据，这个噬能生命体现在处于一种恒定的、可控的超载状态，在一个类似于死亡和重生的无尽循环中，不断地长出新芽，再不断地爆裂。\n\n我们不知道这个生命体是否有思考能力，但我们希望它的存在本身现在和它产生的光图案一样令人愉快。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1025`

### English

The Kaleidoscope has latched onto the modified Dyson Sphere we provided, consuming the energy of a star and extending its colorful petals to encompass the whole system. According to our data, the energy being is now in a state of constant, controlled overload, perpetually growing new buds and bursting in an endless cycle akin to death and rebirth.\n\nWe do not know if the being is capable of thought, but we hope its existence is now as pleasant as the patterns of light it produces.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1202`

## KALEIDOSCOPE_INFESTATION_FIRST

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AKALEIDOSCOPE_INFESTATION_FIRST&lang=en)

### 中文

能量停供

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1028`

### English

Power Outage

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1207`

## KALEIDOSCOPE_INFESTATION_FIRST_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AKALEIDOSCOPE_INFESTATION_FIRST_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

能源电网的全球性故障使我们的首都陷入了完全的黑暗。我们必须恢复§H[root.GetName]§!的电力基础设施，并压实相应责任主体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1029`

### English

A global failure of the energy grid has plunged our capital into total darkness. We must restore §H[root.GetName]§!'s power infrastructure and determine what is taking place.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1208`

## KALEIDOSCOPE_INFESTATION

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AKALEIDOSCOPE_INFESTATION&lang=en)

### 中文

流光溢彩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1031`

### English

Color Bleed

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1211`

## KALEIDOSCOPE_INFESTATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AKALEIDOSCOPE_INFESTATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

又一次，万华镜寄生虫锁死了我们的电网，消耗我们的能源供给，使行星陷入黑暗。\n\n幸运的是，我们现在知道如何应对。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1032`

### English

Once again, the Kaleidoscope has latched to our power grid, consuming our energy and plunging the planet into darkness.\n\nFortunately, we now know how to respond.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1212`

## operation_kaleidoscope

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_kaleidoscope&lang=en)

### 中文

引诱万华镜寄生虫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1034`

### English

Lure the Kaleidoscope

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1215`

## operation_kaleidoscope_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_kaleidoscope_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过在另一个帝国的能源区划埋下一枚返航信标，我们能够将这个噬能生命体带到另一个星际帝国去祸害当地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1035`

### English

By planting a homing beacon in another empire's energy districts, we can send the energy being to infect another galactic power.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1216`

## operation_kaleidoscope_shielded_world

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_kaleidoscope_shielded_world&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的首都星球受§Y$building_planetary_shield_generator$§!保护。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1036`

### English

Their capital world is protected by a §Y$building_planetary_shield_generator$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1217`

## operation_kaleidoscope_previous_owner

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_kaleidoscope_previous_owner&lang=en)

### 中文

他们不会让我们这么快再次突破他们的安保。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1037`

### English

They won't let us get past their security again so soon.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1218`

## operation_kaleidoscope_on_success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_kaleidoscope_on_success&lang=en)

### 中文

$IF_SUCCESSFUL|E$\n万华镜寄生虫将被带至[root.target.GetName]，侵染他们的首都。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/situations_l_simp_chinese.yml:1038`

### English

$IF_SUCCESSFUL|E$\nThe Kaleidoscope will be passed to [root.target.GetName], infecting their capital.

Source: `localisation/english/situations_l_english.yml:1219`
